猴痘病例再次上升,特考韦瑞治疗失败和巴西采购25万剂疫苗

Casos de mpox voltam a subir, antiviral falha em testes e Brasil compra 25 mil doses da vacina
作者： Klinger Soares Faico Filho, Klinger Filho
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
流行病涉及巴西 猴痘 的情况和疫苗功效以及治疗措施,特别是新病毒传播的情况。
设计与对象
我们对里约热内卢和圣保罗的 猴痘 流行病学分析进行了研究,以了解有关疫苗 MVA-BN 和 抗病毒药物 特考韦瑞 临床的信息,包括不同分支之间的比较做病毒。
核心方法
利用论坛通知、病毒鉴定、观察研究和疫苗功效临床分析以及 抗病毒药物 特考韦瑞 的遗传分析。
主要结果
里约热内卢的 74 个 猴痘 注册论坛中,有 43 个 62 个 amostras coletadas entre setembro 和 outubro do clado Ib。特考韦瑞 与 clado II 的情况相比,最有效,与 clado IIb 的感染风险相比,MVA-BN 疫苗可减少 76% 和 82% 的感染风险。
解释与创新
巴西 猴痘 的情况报告与 Ib 循环有关,为了有效对抗抗病毒测试,需要制定预防和控制疫苗和警戒的策略流行病学。
科研与公卫意义
MVA-BN 疫苗的结果对 猴痘 的打击非常重要,特别是针对 HIV 免疫和感染的弱势群体,因此有必要进行改革,以继续监测新病毒的传播。
局限与证据强度
特考韦瑞 是基于观察研究和临床研究的疫苗功效,它限制了特定分支的范围,并减少了结果的概括。证据温和。

Casos de mpox voltam a subir, antiviral falha em testes e Brasil compra 25 mil doses da vacina

Authors: Klinger Soares Faico Filho, Klinger Filho
Original abstract (English)
Nos últimos dois meses, a cidade do Rio de Janeiro registrou 74 casos de mpox, doença infecciosa causada pelo vírus monkeypox (MPXV). As lesões na pele são o sinal mais comum da doença. Elas também podem aparecer na boca, nos genitais ou na região anal. Outros sintomas incluem febre, ínguas, dor de cabeça, dores no corpo, calafrios e fraqueza. O número de casos na capital fluminense é sete vezes maior do que a quantidade apontada no mesmo período em 2025. No estado de São Paulo, 67 casos foram confirmados desde 4 de outubro, o que representa mais de um quinto de todos os casos da doença que o estado notificou neste ano. Os números nacionais não assustam: 832 casos e um óbito até 6 de novembro, contra cerca de 10,5 mil casos e 14 mortes em 2022. A média, porém, esconde o que realmente importa: a curva voltou a subir em setembro, com concentração em duas capitais e circulação de uma linhagem do vírus diferente daquela identificada no surto anterior. Atualmente, o clado Ib predomina entre as amostras analisadas. O surto de mpox de 2022 foi causado pelo clado IIb. Em virologia, um clado é um grupo de linhagens virais que compartilham um ancestral comum. Em resposta, o Ministério da Saúde comprou 25 mil doses da vacina contra a doença e orientou estados e municípios a aplicá-la por via intradérmica, técnica que pode multiplicar por cinco o número de pessoas vacinadas com o mesmo frasco. A vacina ganhou ainda mais importância no enfrentamento da mpox porque o único antiviral disponível para tratar a doença acaba de ser reprovado em dois ensaios clínicos. O que mudou no vírus O clado Ib, que predomina entre as amostras analisadas no Rio, foi identificado em 2023 no leste da República Democrática do Congo e levou a Organização Mundial da Saúde (OMS) a declarar emergência inter...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
A pesquisa busca entender a recorrência de casos de mpox no Brasil e a eficácia da vacina e dos tratamentos disponíveis, especialmente diante da circulação de uma nova linhagem do vírus.
Design and population
O estudo analisa dados epidemiológicos de casos de mpox no Rio de Janeiro e em São Paulo, bem como informações sobre a vacina MVA-BN e os ensaios clínicos com o antiviral tecovirimat, incluindo comparações entre diferentes clados do vírus.
Core methods
Foram utilizados dados de notificação de casos, análise de amostras genéticas para identificar clados do vírus e estudos observacionais e clínicos para avaliar a eficácia da vacina e do antiviral tecovirimat.
Main results
No Rio de Janeiro, 74 casos de mpox foram registrados nos últimos dois meses, sendo 43 das 62 amostras coletadas entre setembro e outubro do clado Ib. O tecovirimat não mostrou eficácia significativa no tratamento de casos do clado II, e a vacina MVA-BN foi associada a uma redução estimada de 76% a 82% do risco de infecção com o clado IIb.
Interpretation and novelty
A recorrência de casos de mpox no Brasil está relacionada à circulação do clado Ib, que não foi efetivamente combatida pelos antivirais testados, reforçando a necessidade de estratégias de prevenção e controle baseadas na vacinação e na vigilância epidemiológica.
Scientific and public-health significance
Os resultados destacam a importância da vacina MVA-BN no combate à mpox, especialmente em grupos vulneráveis como imunossuprimidos e pessoas com HIV, e reforçam a necessidade de monitoramento contínuo da circulação de novas linhagens do vírus.
Limitations and evidence strength
A eficácia da vacina foi baseada em estudos observacionais, e os ensaios clínicos com tecovirimat foram limitados a clados específicos, o que reduz a generalização dos resultados. A evidência é moderada.

MicroDUET:病原体 监测 的双头基因组基础模型

MicroDUET: a dual-headed genomic foundation model for pathogen surveillance
作者： Michael Barber, Carl J. E. Suster, Jingyi Lei, Marya Getchell, Suci Melati Wulandari, Vitali Sintchenko, Paul Pronyk
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决传统 基因组监测 方法的局限性,这些方法大规模比较速度慢且成本高。The authors highlighted the need for a more efficient approach to handle the increasing volume of pathogen genomic data.
设计与对象
该研究引入了 MicroDUET(一种双头基因组基础模型)作为病原体 监测 的新方法。它评估了模型在保留检索基准和独立抗菌素耐药性等级基准上的性能。
核心方法
MicroDUET was developed by adapting a 26.6-million-parameter ProkBERT backbone with two 3.5-million-parameter adapter heads. One head was trained contrastively on genome windows from the same microbes to resolve evolutionary ancestry, while the other was trained to identify genes across taxa to assess phenotypic capability.
主要结果
在保留的检索基准上,系统发育 头的细胞查询准确率达到 80.3%,病毒查询准确率达到 66.6%,而功能头的基因家族识别准确率达到 97.3% 和 97.2%。MicroDUET 在独立的抗菌素耐药性等级基准上显示出 74.4% 的准确度。
解释与创新
作者将这些结果解释为 MicroDUET 与现有基因组编码器相比具有优越性能的证据。他们指出,该模型无需事先接受这些任务训练即可识别谱系和进化枝的能力代表了病原体 监测 的一项新进展。
科研与公卫意义
MicroDUET 有潜力显着提高 基因组监测 对于 猴痘病毒 和其他病原体的可扩展性和效率。它可以帮助快速识别新出现的谱系和耐药模式,支持及时的公共卫生应对措施。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象证据,可能无法捕获模型性能的全部范围。然而,坚持的基准测试结果表明,人们对 MicroDUET 针对病原体 监测 的能力充满信心。

MicroDUET: a dual-headed genomic foundation model for pathogen surveillance

Authors: Michael Barber, Carl J. E. Suster, Jingyi Lei, Marya Getchell, Suci Melati Wulandari, Vitali Sintchenko, Paul Pronyk
Original abstract (English)
Pathogen genomic surveillance works from raw reads, assembled genomes and alignments, representations that are slow and costly to compare at scale. Compact embeddings from genomic language models offer an alternative, but surveillance requires resolving both evolutionary ancestry (phylogeny) and phenotypic capability (function) - which need not co-vary. Here we present MicroDUET, which adapts a 26.6-million-parameter ProkBERT backbone with two 3.5-million-parameter adapter heads: a phylogeny head trained contrastively on genome windows from the same microbes, and a function head trained to identify genes across taxa. On held-out retrieval benchmarks, the phylogeny head returned the correct species for 80.3% and 66.6% of cellular and viral queries, and the function head the correct gene family for 97.3% and 97.2%, reaching 74.4% accuracy on an independent antimicrobial resistance-class benchmark. Across all three benchmarks MicroDUET outranked 17 other genomic encoders. Without training on lineage, clade or drug-susceptibility tasks, MicroDUET identified Mycobacterium tuberculosis and mpox virus lineages and clades (0.965, 0.975 accuracy), discriminated rifampicin resistance without lineage adjustment (AUROC 0.985), and retrospectively identified an emerging mpox lineage as anomalous (AUROC 0.974). MicroDUET operates 10^3 - 10^4 times faster than single-threaded sequence-based alignments. MicroDUET therefore provides a compact, reusable representation for scalable pathogen surveillance and sequence triage.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the limitations of traditional genomic surveillance methods, which are slow and costly to compare at scale. The authors highlighted the need for a more efficient approach to handle the increasing volume of pathogen genomic data.
Design and population
The study introduced MicroDUET, a dual-headed genomic foundation model, as a novel approach to pathogen surveillance. It evaluated the model's performance on held-out retrieval benchmarks and independent antimicrobial resistance-class benchmarks.
Core methods
MicroDUET was developed by adapting a 26.6-million-parameter ProkBERT backbone with two 3.5-million-parameter adapter heads. One head was trained contrastively on genome windows from the same microbes to resolve evolutionary ancestry, while the other was trained to identify genes across taxa to assess phenotypic capability.
Main results
On held-out retrieval benchmarks, the phylogeny head achieved 80.3% accuracy for cellular queries and 66.6% for viral queries, while the function head reached 97.3% and 97.2% accuracy for gene family identification. MicroDUET demonstrated 74.4% accuracy on an independent antimicrobial resistance-class benchmark.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these results as evidence of MicroDUET's superior performance compared to existing genomic encoders. They noted that the model's ability to identify lineages and clades without prior training on these tasks represents a novel advancement in pathogen surveillance.
Scientific and public-health significance
MicroDUET has the potential to significantly improve the scalability and efficiency of genomic surveillance for mpox virus and other pathogens. It can aid in rapid identification of emerging lineages and resistance patterns, supporting timely public health responses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-only evidence, which may not capture the full scope of the model's performance. However, the results on held-out benchmarks suggest a high level of confidence in MicroDUET's capabilities for pathogen surveillance.

Viro-BERT:从下一代测序数据中对稳健病毒病原体分类进行整体 基因组 关注。

Viro-BERT: Whole-Genome Attention for Robust Viral Pathogen Classification from Next-Generation Sequencing Data.
作者： Sami Dhahbi, Anas Bilal, Talha Mahboob Alam, Mouhebeddine Berrima, Amina Magdich, Hamdy Morsy
摘要中文翻译根据下一代测序 (NGS) 数据对病毒病原体进行分类是 诊断 和 基因组监测 的核心。然而,大多数深度学习分类器只读取每个 基因组 开头的固定窗口,通常是几百到几千个碱基对,因此忽略了 95% 以上的大型 DNA 病毒序列,例如猴痘病毒 (MPV) 和单纯疱疹病毒 1 (HSV-1)。我们将这个问题称为截断盲性。在这里,我们提出了 Viro-BERT,它将完整的 基因组 分割成重叠的 512 个令牌窗口,使用共享的 DistilBERT 编码器对每个窗口进行编码,并在后期融合共识层中对窗口级类别概率进行平均。由于窗口是独立处理的,因此内存使用量不会随着 基因组 长度的增加而增加。我们对 14 个病毒类别使用相同的分割和训练协议,将 Viro-BERT 与一维卷积神经网络 (1D-CNN)、双向长短期记忆 (Bi-LSTM) 和 RoBERTa 基线进行比较。Viro-BERT 的准确率达到 96.79%,宏观 F1 得分为 96.17%。然后我们将随机替换噪声添加到测试 基因组 中。在 5% 的噪声下,1D-CNN 的准确率下降了 5.57 个百分点,而 Viro-BERT 仍然正确分类了 93.68% 的 基因组。HSV-1 的位置和注意力分析表明,Viro-BERT 使用来自 基因组 两端的诊断信号,而不是仅来自其 5' 端。这些结果表明,病毒分类器需要完整的 基因组 上下文,才能在嘈杂的临床数据上保持可靠。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
根据下一代测序 (NGS) 数据对病毒病原体进行分类是 诊断 和 基因组监测 的核心。
设计与对象
然而,大多数深度学习分类器只读取每个 基因组 开头的固定窗口,通常是几百到几千个碱基对,因此忽略了 95% 以上的大型 DNA 病毒序列,例如猴痘病毒 (MPV) 和单纯疱疹病毒 1 (HSV-1)。
核心方法
我们将这个问题称为截断盲性。在这里,我们提出了 Viro-BERT,它将完整的 基因组 分割成重叠的 512 个令牌窗口,使用共享的 DistilBERT 编码器对每个窗口进行编码,并在后期融合共识层中对窗口级类别概率进行平均。
主要结果
Viro-BERT 的准确率达到 96.79%,宏观 F1 得分为 96.17%。在 5% 的噪声下,1D-CNN 的准确率下降了 5.57 个百分点,而 Viro-BERT 仍然正确分类了 93.68% 的 基因组。
解释与创新
这些结果表明,病毒分类器需要完整的 基因组 上下文,才能在嘈杂的临床数据上保持可靠。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,Viro-BERT 可以提高测序数据可能存在噪音或不完整的临床环境中病毒分类的可靠性。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计的具体局限性。

Viro-BERT: Whole-Genome Attention for Robust Viral Pathogen Classification from Next-Generation Sequencing Data.

Authors: Sami Dhahbi, Anas Bilal, Talha Mahboob Alam, Mouhebeddine Berrima, Amina Magdich, Hamdy Morsy
Original abstract (English)
Classifying viral pathogens from next-generation sequencing (NGS) data is central to diagnosis and genomic surveillance. Most deep learning classifiers, however, read only a fixed window at the start of each genome, typically a few hundred to a few thousand base pairs, and so ignore more than 95% of the sequence of large DNA viruses such as monkeypox virus (MPV) and herpes simplex virus 1 (HSV-1). We refer to this problem as Truncation Blindness. Here we present Viro-BERT, which splits a complete genome into overlapping 512-token windows, encodes each window with a shared DistilBERT encoder, and averages the window-level class probabilities in a Late Fusion Consensus layer. Because the windows are processed independently, memory use does not grow with genome length. We compared Viro-BERT with one-dimensional convolutional neural network (1D-CNN), bidirectional long short-term memory (Bi-LSTM) and RoBERTa baselines on 14 viral classes, using the same segmentation and training protocol for all models. Viro-BERT reached 96.79% accuracy and a macro-F1 score of 96.17%. We then added random substitution noise to the test genomes. At 5% noise, the accuracy of the 1D-CNN fell by 5.57 percentage points, whereas Viro-BERT still classified 93.68% of genomes correctly. Positional and attention analyses on HSV-1 showed that Viro-BERT uses diagnostic signal from across the genome rather than from its 5' end alone. These results indicate that whole-genome context is needed for viral classifiers that remain reliable on noisy clinical data.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Classifying viral pathogens from next-generation sequencing (NGS) data is central to diagnosis and genomic surveillance.
Design and population
Most deep learning classifiers, however, read only a fixed window at the start of each genome, typically a few hundred to a few thousand base pairs, and so ignore more than 95% of the sequence of large DNA viruses such as monkeypox virus (MPV) and herpes simplex virus 1 (HSV-1).
Core methods
We refer to this problem as Truncation Blindness. Here we present Viro-BERT, which splits a complete genome into overlapping 512-token windows, encodes each window with a shared DistilBERT encoder, and averages the window-level class probabilities in a Late Fusion Consensus layer.
Main results
Viro-BERT reached 96.79% accuracy and a macro-F1 score of 96.17%. At 5% noise, the accuracy of the 1D-CNN fell by 5.57 percentage points, whereas Viro-BERT still classified 93.68% of genomes correctly.
Interpretation and novelty
These results indicate that whole-genome context is needed for viral classifiers that remain reliable on noisy clinical data.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that Viro-BERT can improve the reliability of viral classification in clinical settings where sequencing data may be noisy or incomplete.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study design.

韩国一家三级医疗机构中分离的Candidozyma auris菌株的全基因组特征:II类菌株占主导地位、部分无法解释的氟康唑耐药性以及I类菌株

Whole-genome characterization of Candidozyma auris isolates from a Korean tertiary care centre: clade II predominance, partly unexplained fluconazole resistance and a clade I isolate
作者： Jae Hyeon Park, Jee-Soo Lee, Seung Won Chae, Taek Soo Kim, Moon-Woo Seong
摘要中文翻译在韩国,Candidozyma auris主要由与耳部相关的II类菌株组成,但基于机构的基因组数据仍较为有限。我们分析了2017年至2023年间在一家三级医疗机构中从29名患者分离的35株存档菌株,采用抗真菌药物敏感性试验和全基因组测序。其中34株属于II类菌株,而一株通过医院间转移获得的尿液分离株属于子类Ib。来自同一患者的连续II类菌株聚类在一起,其成对SNP距离低于不同患者之间的菌株(中位数,8.5 vs 181)。在29名患者的首次分离株中,有14株(48.3%)表现出氟康唑耐药性。在首次分离的II类菌株中,反复出现的编码变异主要集中在TAC1B和ERG11基因;TAC1B F214S和W198C以及ERG11 K143R仅出现在耐药菌株中,但已知的编码变异并未解释所有耐药菌株。II类菌株共享一个广泛保守的亚端粒区缺失骨架,但染色体6上某些糖基磷脂酰肌醇锚定基因的缺失情况存在差异。该Ib类菌株携带ERG11 Y132F、TAC1B A583S以及类似CDR1 V704L/E709D的变异,并符合CDC暂定的氟康唑、两性霉素B和卡泊芬净耐药性标准。这些发现细化了韩国II类菌株的基因组特征,并突出了通过医疗传播可能引入I类菌株的潜在可能性,这可能对理解耐药机制具有重要意义。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在描述韩国Candidozyma auris菌株的基因组特征,并探讨氟康唑耐药性的机制,特别是针对II类菌株,以应对目前基于机构的基因组数据有限的问题。
设计与对象
本研究分析了2017年至2023年间在一家三级医疗机构中从29名患者分离的35株存档菌株,包括II类菌株和一株通过医院间转移获得的Ib类菌株。
核心方法
采用全基因组测序和抗真菌药物敏感性试验对菌株进行分析,重点在于识别基因组变异和耐药模式。
主要结果
34 个分离株属于分支 II,而来自另一名患者的尿液分离株属于分支 Ib。来自同一患者的连续进化枝 II 分离株比来自不同患者的连续进化枝 II 分离株具有更低的成对 SNP 距离。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了韩国分支 II 分离株的优势,并表明通过医疗保健转移可能引入独特的分支 I 谱系,这可能对耐药机制产生影响。
科研与公卫意义
该研究深入了解了韩国耳念珠菌的基因组多样性和耐药模式,可为 监测 和医疗机构中的感染控制策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于存档的分离株,这可能会限制对近期趋势或 传播 动态得出结论的能力,并且证据仅限于基因组和易感性数据。

Whole-genome characterization of Candidozyma auris isolates from a Korean tertiary care centre: clade II predominance, partly unexplained fluconazole resistance and a clade I isolate

Authors: Jae Hyeon Park, Jee-Soo Lee, Seung Won Chae, Taek Soo Kim, Moon-Woo Seong
Original abstract (English)
In South Korea, Candidozyma auris is dominated by ear-associated clade II isolates, but institution-based genomic data remain limited. We analysed 35 archived isolates from 29 patients at a tertiary care centre between 2017 and 2023 using antifungal susceptibility testing and whole-genome sequencing. Thirty-four isolates belonged to clade II, and one urine isolate recovered after inter-hospital transfer belonged to subclade Ib. Serial clade II isolates from the same patient clustered together and had lower pairwise SNP distances than different-patient pairs (median, 8.5 versus 181). Among the first isolates from 29 patients, 14 (48.3%) were fluconazole resistant. In first clade II isolates, recurrent curated coding variation was concentrated in TAC1B and ERG11 ; TAC1B F214S and W198C and ERG11 K143R occurred only in resistant isolates, but recognized coding variants did not account for all resistant isolates. Clade II isolates shared a broadly conserved subtelomeric deletion backbone, with variable chromosome 6 loss of selected glycosylphosphatidylinositol-anchor genes. The clade Ib isolate carried ERG11 Y132F, TAC1B A583S and a CDR1 V704L/E709D-like change and met CDC tentative breakpoints for resistance to fluconazole, amphotericin B and caspofungin. These findings refine the genomic profile of Korean clade II isolates and highlight the potential introduction of a distinct clade I lineage through healthcare transfer.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to characterize the genomic profile of Candidozyma auris isolates in South Korea and investigate the prevalence of fluconazole resistance, particularly in clade II isolates, to address the limited institution-based genomic data available.
Design and population
The study analyzed 35 archived isolates from 29 patients at a tertiary care centre between 2017 and 2023, including both clade II and a single clade Ib isolate recovered after inter-hospital transfer.
Core methods
Whole-genome sequencing and antifungal susceptibility testing were used to analyze the isolates, with a focus on identifying genomic variations and resistance patterns.
Main results
Thirty-four isolates belonged to clade II, while one urine isolate from a different patient belonged to subclade Ib. Serial clade II isolates from the same patient had lower pairwise SNP distances than those from different patients.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the predominance of clade II isolates in South Korea and suggest the potential introduction of a distinct clade I lineage through healthcare transfer, which may have implications for resistance mechanisms.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study provides insights into the genomic diversity and resistance patterns of Candidozyma auris in South Korea, which can inform surveillance and infection control strategies in healthcare settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on archived isolates, which may limit the ability to draw conclusions about recent trends or transmission dynamics, and the evidence is limited to genomic and susceptibility data.

传播 模式和 猴痘 的未来预测:预测和公共卫生准备的数学建模方法综述

Transmission patterns and future forecasting of mpox: a review of mathematical modelling approaches for prediction and public health preparedness
作者： Noor Allehyani, Abdullah Alghamdi, Mohammed A. Alshehri, Ghadah Salim Abusalim, Tawfiq N. Juraybi, Awaji Y. Safhi, Meshari A. Alsuwat, Mohammed Alissa
摘要中文翻译摘要 猴痘 已从主要与中非和西非人畜共患 跨物种溢出 相关的疾病转变为反复出现的全球公共卫生威胁,涉及性接触网络、家庭 传播、国际流动性、生态持久性和进化枝特定的不确定性。数学模型阐明了 2022 clade IIb 暴发 中的重要机制,包括重尾性接触分布、行为适应、疫苗接种 和病例隔离的作用。然而,在对准备工作至关重要的一个方面,文献仍然不太成熟:将 传播 洞察转化为可靠的、针对具体情况的预测。这篇叙述性综述综合了关于 猴痘 传播 模式的同行评审的建模证据,并评估了现有的区室模型、随机模型、网络模型、基于代理的模型、系统动力学模型和时间序列模型支持未来 暴发 预测的程度。该审查确定了四个反复出现的差距:前瞻性验证不足、空间和跨区域滞后的整合薄弱、分支 I/Ib 风险的特定分支参数化有限,以及免疫、报告延迟和 监测 不确定性的纳入不足。我们提出了一个以预测为导向的框架,将流行病学状态变量、接触结构、人群免疫力、流动性、干预措施和不确定性量化与决策相关输出联系起来。这篇综述的新颖之处在于将 猴痘 模型从回顾性解释重新定位为预测准备。预测优先的方法可以加强早期预警、有针对性的 疫苗接种、沟通规划和卫生系统准备工作,特别是在 监测 延迟、不完整或地理分散的环境中。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
猴痘 已从主要与中非和西非的人畜共患 跨物种溢出 相关的疾病转变为经常性的全球公共卫生威胁,涉及性接触网络、家庭 传播、国际流动性、生态持久性和进化枝特定的不确定性。
证据基础与方法
这篇叙述性综述综合了关于 猴痘 传播 模式的同行评审的建模证据,并评估了现有的区室模型、随机模型、网络模型、基于代理的模型、系统动力学模型和时间序列模型支持未来 暴发 预测的程度。
共识与结论
数学模型阐明了 2022 clade IIb 暴发 中的重要机制,包括重尾性接触分布、行为适应、疫苗接种 和病例隔离的作用。
争议与缺口
该审查确定了四个反复出现的差距:前瞻性验证不足、空间和跨区域滞后的整合薄弱、分支 I/Ib 风险的特定分支参数化有限,以及免疫、报告延迟和 监测 不确定性的纳入不足。
科研与实践启示
预测优先的方法可以加强早期预警、有针对性的 疫苗接种、沟通规划和卫生系统准备工作,特别是在 监测 延迟、不完整或地理分散的环境中。

Transmission patterns and future forecasting of mpox: a review of mathematical modelling approaches for prediction and public health preparedness

Authors: Noor Allehyani, Abdullah Alghamdi, Mohammed A. Alshehri, Ghadah Salim Abusalim, Tawfiq N. Juraybi, Awaji Y. Safhi, Meshari A. Alsuwat, Mohammed Alissa
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Mpox has shifted from a disease mainly associated with zoonotic spillover in Central and West Africa to a recurrent global public-health threat involving sexual-contact networks, household transmission, international mobility, ecological persistence and clade-specific uncertainty. Mathematical models have clarified important mechanisms in the 2022 clade IIb outbreak, including the role of heavy-tailed sexual-contact distributions, behavioural adaptation, vaccination and case isolation. However, the literature remains less mature in one respect that is essential for preparedness: the conversion of transmission insight into reliable, setting-specific forecasts. This narrative review synthesizes peer-reviewed modelling evidence on mpox transmission patterns and evaluates the extent to which existing compartmental, stochastic, network, agent-based, phylodynamic and time-series models support future outbreak prediction. The review identifies four recurring gaps: insufficient prospective validation, weak integration of spatial and cross-regional lags, limited clade-specific parameterization for clade I/Ib risk, and inadequate incorporation of immunity, reporting delay and surveillance uncertainty. We propose a forecasting-oriented framework that links epidemiological state variables, contact structure, population immunity, mobility, interventions and uncertainty quantification to decision-relevant outputs. The novelty of this review lies in repositioning mpox modelling from retrospective explanation toward predictive readiness. A forecasting-first approach can strengthen early warning, targeted vaccination, communication planning and health-system preparedness, particularly in settings where surveillance is delayed, incomplete or geographically fragmented.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Mpox has shifted from a disease mainly associated with zoonotic spillover in Central and West Africa to a recurrent global public-health threat involving sexual-contact networks, household transmission, international mobility, ecological persistence and clade-specific uncertainty.
Evidence base and method
This narrative review synthesizes peer-reviewed modelling evidence on mpox transmission patterns and evaluates the extent to which existing compartmental, stochastic, network, agent-based, phylodynamic and time-series models support future outbreak prediction.
Consensus and conclusions
Mathematical models have clarified important mechanisms in the 2022 clade IIb outbreak, including the role of heavy-tailed sexual-contact distributions, behavioural adaptation, vaccination and case isolation.
Controversies and gaps
The review identifies four recurring gaps: insufficient prospective validation, weak integration of spatial and cross-regional lags, limited clade-specific parameterization for clade I/Ib risk, and inadequate incorporation of immunity, reporting delay and surveillance uncertainty.
Research and practice implications
A forecasting-first approach can strengthen early warning, targeted vaccination, communication planning and health-system preparedness, particularly in settings where surveillance is delayed, incomplete or geographically fragmented.

猴痘 在男性中的表现:澳大利亚病例系列

Manifestations of Mpox in Males: An Australian Case Series
作者： Olga Pawlik, Estella Janz-Robinson, Sarah J. Martin
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 猴痘 通常表现为肛门生殖器病变,与男性生殖器溃疡、脓疱和水肿的其他原因造成诊断重叠。识别对于评估阴茎、肛周或直肠症状的临床医生尤其重要,其中延迟的 诊断 可能会导致并发症和 传播。目的 描述澳大利亚病例系列中 猴痘 的男性生殖器和肛门生殖器表现,包括病变形态、合并感染、诊断陷阱和与男性生殖器皮肤病学相关的并发症。方法 我们对 2023 年 7 月 15 日至 2025 年 10 月 1 日期间到当地卫生服务机构就诊的连续 50 名成年人进行了回顾性调查,并通过核酸扩增检测检测出 猴痘 呈阳性。回顾了临床记录、病理结果、免疫记录和可用的病变照片。该分析强调男性生殖器和肛门生殖器的发现。结果所有患者均为男男性行为者,平均年龄38.6岁。肛门生殖器区域是最常受累的解剖区域:25/50 名患者(50%)的阴茎拭子呈阳性,14/50(28%)的直肠拭子呈阳性。表现包括阴茎病变、肛周或直肠症状、直肠炎、局部阴茎或肛周肿胀,并且在 5 名患者中,尽管没有可见的外部病变,但仍检测到直肠 猴痘。病变被描述为脓疱性、溃疡性、结痂性、丘疹性、水疱性、糜烂性或渗出性,并且有不同程度的疼痛、无痛或瘙痒。鉴别诊断包括一期梅毒、生殖器单纯疱疹病毒、传染性软疣和毛囊炎。10 例患者同时患有性传播感染,其中 1 例 猴痘 和单纯疱疹病毒均呈阳性,1 例 猴痘 和梅毒均呈阳性。5名患者需要入院;男性生殖器并发症包括严重直肠炎、并发性传播感染的败血症、阴茎肿胀、慢性阴茎溃疡和需要导尿的尿潴留。结论猴痘是男性生殖器及肛门生殖器疾病的重要病因。在本 队列研究 中,它最常表现为急性阴茎或直肠疾病,但可能类似于更常见的性传播感染,或者罕见的是需要活检以排除恶性肿瘤的慢性阴茎溃疡。男性生殖器皮肤科服务应在高危男性新出现阴茎、肛周或直肠炎表现时考虑 猴痘,特别是当病变为脓疱、溃疡、渗出或与水肿、淋巴结肿大或并发性传播感染相关时。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
猴痘病毒(正痘病毒 猴痘)是目标。该综述重点关注澳大利亚病例系列中 猴痘 的男性生殖器和肛门生殖器表现,包括病变形态、合并感染、诊断陷阱和与男性生殖器皮肤病学相关的并发症。
证据基础与方法
该综述是对 2023 年 7 月 15 日至 2025 年 10 月 1 日期间通过核酸扩增检测呈 猴痘 阳性的 50 名连续成年人的回顾性病例系列分析。对临床记录、病理结果、免疫记录和可用病变照片进行了审查。研究人群包括男男性行为者,平均年龄为 38.6 岁。肛门生殖器区域是最常受累的解剖区域,25/50(50%)的患者阴茎拭子呈阳性,14/50(28%)的直肠拭子呈阳性。
共识与结论
猴痘是男性生殖器和肛门生殖器疾病的重要原因。它最常表现为急性阴茎或直肠疾病,但可以模仿更常见的性传播感染,或者罕见地,慢性阴茎溃疡,需要活检以排除恶性肿瘤。病变被描述为脓疱性、溃疡性、结痂性、丘疹性、水疱性、糜烂性或渗出性,并且有不同程度的疼痛、无痛或瘙痒。男性生殖器皮肤科服务应在高危男性新出现阴茎、肛周或直肠炎表现时考虑 猴痘,特别是当病变为脓疱、溃疡、渗出或与水肿、淋巴结肿大或并发性传播感染相关时。
争议与缺口
该综述强调了与男性生殖器溃疡、脓疱和水肿的其他原因的诊断重叠,例如一期梅毒、生殖器单纯疱疹病毒、传染性软疣和毛囊炎。没有提及用于区分 猴痘 和这些情况的具体诊断方法或工具。该综述还指出,尽管没有可见的外部病变,但仍有 5 名患者检测到直肠 猴痘,这表明临床检测方法的潜在局限性。证据并未报告任何干预措施或 治疗 对 猴痘 相关并发症的有效性。
科研与实践启示
在外部病变不可见的情况下,需要改进的诊断方法来识别 猴痘,并且需要进一步的研究来了解各种并发症及其治疗。

Manifestations of Mpox in Males: An Australian Case Series

Authors: Olga Pawlik, Estella Janz-Robinson, Sarah J. Martin
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Mpox commonly presents with anogenital lesions, creating diagnostic overlap with other causes of male genital ulceration, pustulation and oedema. Recognition is particularly relevant for clinicians assessing penile, perianal or proctitic symptoms, where delayed diagnosis may result in complications and transmission. Objectives To describe the male genital and anogenital manifestations of mpox in an Australian case series, including lesion morphology, co-infections, diagnostic pitfalls and complications relevant to male genital dermatology. Methods We performed a retrospective review of 50 consecutive adults who presented to local health services between 15 July 2023 and 1 October 2025 and tested positive for mpox by nucleic acid amplification testing. Clinical notes, pathology results, immunisation records and available lesion photographs were reviewed. This analysis emphasises male genital and anogenital findings. Results All patients were men who have sex with men, with a mean age of 38.6 years. The anogenital region was the most frequently involved anatomical area: penile swabs were positive in 25/50 patients (50%) and rectal swabs in 14/50 (28%). Presentations included penile lesions, perianal or rectal symptoms, proctitis, localised penile or perianal swelling and, in five patients, rectal mpox detected despite no visible external lesions. Lesions were described as pustular, ulcerated, crusted, papular, vesicular, erosive or exudative, and were variably painful, painless or pruritic. Differential diagnoses included primary syphilis, genital herpes simplex virus, molluscum contagiosum and folliculitis. Ten patients had concurrent sexually transmitted infections, including one lesion positive for both mpox and herpes simplex virus and one lesion positive for both mpox and syphilis. Five patients required hospital admission; male genital complications included severe proctitis, sepsis with concurrent sexually transmitted infection, penile swelling, chronic penile ulceration and urinary retention requiring catheterisation. Conclusions Mpox is an important cause of male genital and anogenital disease. In this cohort it most often presented as acute penile or rectal disease, but could mimic more common sexually transmitted infections or, rarely, a chronic penile ulcer requiring biopsy to exclude malignancy. Male genital dermatology services should consider mpox in new penile, perianal or proctitic presentations in at-risk men, particularly where lesions are pustular, ulcerated, exudative or associated with oedema, lymphadenopathy or concurrent sexually transmitted infection.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Mpox virus (Orthopoxvirus monkeypox) is the target. The review focuses on the male genital and anogenital manifestations of mpox in an Australian case series, including lesion morphology, co-infections, diagnostic pitfalls and complications relevant to male genital dermatology.
Evidence base and method
The review is a retrospective case series analysis of 50 consecutive adults who tested positive for mpox by nucleic acid amplification testing between 15 July 2023 and 1 October 2025. Clinical notes, pathology results, immunisation records and available lesion photographs were reviewed. The study population includes men who have sex with men, with a mean age of 38.6 years. The anogenital region was the most frequently involved anatomical area, with penile swabs positive in 25/50 patients (50%) and rectal swabs in 14/50 (28%).
Consensus and conclusions
Mpox is an important cause of male genital and anogenital disease. It most often presents as acute penile or rectal disease but can mimic more common sexually transmitted infections or, rarely, a chronic penile ulcer requiring biopsy to exclude malignancy. Lesions were described as pustular, ulcerated, crusted, papular, vesicular, erosive or exudative, and were variably painful, painless or pruritic. Male genital dermatology services should consider mpox in new penile, perianal or proctitic presentations in at-risk men, particularly where lesions are pustular, ulcerated, exudative or associated with oedema, lymphadenopathy or concurrent sexually transmitted infection.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights diagnostic overlap with other causes of male genital ulceration, pustulation and oedema, such as primary syphilis, genital herpes simplex virus, molluscum contagiosum and folliculitis. There is no mention of specific diagnostic methods or tools for distinguishing mpox from these conditions. The review also notes that five patients had rectal mpox detected despite no visible external lesions, indicating potential limitations in clinical detection methods. The evidence does not report on the effectiveness of any interventions or treatments for mpox-related complications.
Research and practice implications
There is a need for improved diagnostic methods to identify mpox in cases where external lesions are not visible, and further research is required to understand the full range of complications and their management.

尼日利亚母胎猴痘在多中心病例系列中的诊断挑战、传播途径及新生儿结局

Maternal and neonatal mpox in Nigeria: a multicentre case series highlighting diagnostic challenges, transmission pathways, and neonatal outcomes
作者： Chizaram Onyeaghala, Ebenovbe Idemudia, Victoria Eno Gomba, Obinna Tobechukwu Okonyia
摘要中文翻译妊娠期和新生儿期的 猴痘 特征仍不充分,特别是在非洲流行地区。孕产妇感染可能无法被识别,而新生儿疾病可能很早就出现并需要长期的临床护理。我们描述了在尼日利亚南部管理的三对母婴,重点介绍了母亲的认知、可能的 传播 途径、新生儿表现和结果。我们对 2025 年 1 月至 5 月期间在尼日利亚南部三家医院管理的三名实验室确诊的 猴痘 新生儿进行了回顾性病例系列。这些病例是在常规临床护理中识别新生儿水疱脓疱病并通过病灶拭子实时聚合酶链反应 (RT-PCR) 确认猴痘病毒 (MPXV) 感染后发现的。对母亲的临床病史、流行病学信息、调查、治疗和结果进行了回顾性审查。两人在妊娠晚期出现发烧和水疱脓疱疹,而第三人在分娩前一天出现短暂的发热性疾病,但没有发现皮疹。产妇 猴痘 在分娩前未被识别,并且所有妇女均未接受 MPXV 检测。两名父亲在离开河流州旅行后患有相似的疾病,这提供了可能的额外流行病学联系。所有三名男性新生儿均在出生后第 4 天至第 9 天出现发烧和播散性水疱脓疱疹,并在第 14 天至第 16 天期间入院。RT-PCR 检测新生儿病灶拭子 MPXV 呈阳性(循环阈值 19.70-21.60),而水痘带状疱疹病毒 PCR 结果呈阴性。管理层主要是支持的。特考韦瑞 曾被考虑,但在研究期间无法在尼日利亚用于临床。两名新生儿已完全康复。第三名患者在住院治疗中断后于出生第 24 天在家中死亡。没有进行尸检;因此,无法确定直接死亡原因。现有证据无法确定新生儿感染途径。这些病例表明,在新生儿疾病促使调查之前,与 猴痘 相符的孕产妇疾病可能仍然未被识别。早期新生儿表现、传播 通路的不确定性以及维持住院护理的挑战凸显了母婴界面 猴痘 识别和管理方面的重要差距。改善临床怀疑、及时分子 诊断 和连续护理是流行病环境中的重要优先事项。

范围与问题
妊娠期和新生儿期的 猴痘 特征仍不充分,特别是在非洲流行地区。我们描述了在尼日利亚南部管理的三对母婴,重点介绍了母亲的认知、可能的 传播 途径、新生儿表现和结果。
证据基础与方法
我们对 2025 年 1 月至 5 月期间在尼日利亚南部三家医院管理的三名实验室确诊的 猴痘 新生儿进行了回顾性病例系列。这些病例是在常规临床护理中识别新生儿水疱脓疱病并通过病灶拭子实时聚合酶链反应 (RT-PCR) 确认猴痘病毒 (MPXV) 感染后发现的。对母亲的临床病史、流行病学信息、调查、治疗和结果进行了回顾性审查。
共识与结论
产妇 猴痘 在分娩前未被识别,并且所有妇女均未接受 MPXV 检测。所有三名男性新生儿均在出生后第 4 天至第 9 天出现发烧和播散性水疱脓疱疹,并在第 14 天至第 16 天期间入院。RT-PCR 检测新生儿病灶拭子 MPXV 呈阳性(循环阈值 19.70-21.60),而水痘带状疱疹病毒 PCR 结果呈阴性。管理层主要是支持的。两名新生儿已完全康复。第三名患者在住院治疗中断后于出生第 24 天在家中死亡。没有进行尸检;因此,无法确定直接死亡原因。现有证据无法确定新生儿感染途径。
争议与缺口
早期新生儿表现、传播 通路的不确定性以及维持住院护理的挑战凸显了母婴界面 猴痘 识别和管理方面的重要差距。
科研与实践启示
改善临床怀疑、及时分子 诊断 和连续护理是流行病环境中的重要优先事项。

Maternal and neonatal mpox in Nigeria: a multicentre case series highlighting diagnostic challenges, transmission pathways, and neonatal outcomes

Authors: Chizaram Onyeaghala, Ebenovbe Idemudia, Victoria Eno Gomba, Obinna Tobechukwu Okonyia
Original abstract (English)
Mpox during pregnancy and the neonatal period remains insufficiently characterised, particularly in endemic African settings. Maternal infection may go unrecognised, while neonatal disease can present early and require prolonged clinical care. We describe three mother–infant pairs managed in southern Nigeria, highlighting maternal recognition, possible transmission pathways, neonatal manifestations, and outcomes. We conducted a retrospective case series of three neonates with laboratory-confirmed mpox managed at three hospitals in southern Nigeria between January and May 2025. Cases were identified during routine clinical care following recognition of neonatal vesiculopustular illness and confirmation of monkeypox virus (MPXV) infection by real-time polymerase chain reaction (RT-PCR) on lesion swabs. Maternal clinical histories, epidemiological information, investigations, management, and outcomes were retrospectively reviewed. Two developed fever and vesiculopustular eruptions in the third trimester, while the third had a brief febrile illness without a recognised rash one day before delivery. Maternal mpox was not recognised before delivery, and none of the women underwent MPXV testing. Two fathers had compatible illnesses following travel outside Rivers State, providing possible additional epidemiological links. All three male neonates developed fever and disseminated vesiculopustular eruptions between days 4 and 9 of life and presented to hospital between days 14 and 16. Neonatal lesion swabs were positive for MPXV by RT-PCR (cycle threshold values, 19.70–21.60), while varicella-zoster virus PCR results were negative. Management was primarily supportive. Tecovirimat was considered but was unavailable for clinical use in Nigeria during the study period. Two neonates recovered completely. The third died at home on day 24 of life following interruption of inpatient care. No post-mortem examination was performed; therefore, the immediate cause of death could not be established. The available evidence did not permit determination of the route of neonatal infection. These cases demonstrate how maternal illness compatible with mpox may remain unrecognised until neonatal disease prompts investigation. Early neonatal presentation, uncertainty about transmission pathways, and challenges sustaining inpatient care highlight important gaps in the recognition and management of mpox at the maternal–newborn interface. Improved clinical suspicion, timely molecular diagnosis, and continuity of care are important priorities in endemic settings.

Mpox during pregnancy and the neonatal period remains insufficiently characterised, particularly in endemic African settings. We describe three mother–infant pairs managed in southern Nigeria, highlighting maternal recognition, possible transmission pathways, neonatal manifestations, and outcomes.
Evidence base and method
We conducted a retrospective case series of three neonates with laboratory-confirmed mpox managed at three hospitals in southern Nigeria between January and May 2025. Cases were identified during routine clinical care following recognition of neonatal vesiculopustular illness and confirmation of monkeypox virus (MPXV) infection by real-time polymerase chain reaction (RT-PCR) on lesion swabs. Maternal clinical histories, epidemiological information, investigations, management, and outcomes were retrospectively reviewed.
Consensus and conclusions
Maternal mpox was not recognised before delivery, and none of the women underwent MPXV testing. All three male neonates developed fever and disseminated vesiculopustular eruptions between days 4 and 9 of life and presented to hospital between days 14 and 16. Neonatal lesion swabs were positive for MPXV by RT-PCR (cycle threshold values, 19.70–21.60), while varicella-zoster virus PCR results were negative. Management was primarily supportive. Two neonates recovered completely. The third died at home on day 24 of life following interruption of inpatient care. No post-mortem examination was performed; therefore, the immediate cause of death could not be established. The available evidence did not permit determination of the route of neonatal infection.
Controversies and gaps

Research and practice implications
Improved clinical suspicion, timely molecular diagnosis, and continuity of care are important priorities in endemic settings.

Análise epidemiológica da 猴痘 no século XXI: uma revisão narrativa 21 世纪 猴痘 的流行病学分析:叙述性回顾 Análisis epidemiológico de la 猴痘 en el siglo XXI: una revisión narrativa

Análise epidemiológica da mpox no século XXI: uma revisão narrativa Epidemiological analysis of mpox in the 21st century: a narrative review Análisis epidemiológico de la mpox en el siglo XXI: una revisión narrativa
作者： Carlielma da Costa de Assunção, Pamela Ferreira Marques de Aquino, Samara Maria Cavalcante de Souza, Fabiolla da Silva dos Santos, Andréa Cristina Monteiro dos Santos, Luiz Henrique Campos Holanda, de Assunção Carlielma da Costa, dos Santos Fabiolla da Silva
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本次审查重点关注 猴痘病毒,检查其流行病学 进化、社会人口学和临床概况、传播 模式以及 监测 和控制措施。核心问题是了解 猴痘 的全球传播及其在特定人群中的特征,特别是在巴西。
证据基础与方法
该综述是利用科学数据库和机构文件对 2026 年 2 月至 10 月期间进行的文献进行叙述性分析。它本质上是描述性和定性的,没有明确提及荟萃分析或汇总估计。
共识与结论
这些研究强调了 猴痘 的国际扩张,并指出在巴西,大多数确诊病例和疑似病例是在出生时被指定为男性的个体中报告的。性接触是最常报告的暴露方式,但也注意到了其他密切接触。
争议与缺口
该证据并未报告与 猴痘病毒 相关的具体争议或证据差距。然而,该综述表明需要进一步研究 传播 动态以及不同人群在疾病传播中的作用。
科研与实践启示
研究结果强调了持续流行病学 监测、及时实验室 诊断、接触者追踪、根据现行指南的 疫苗接种 以及避免污名化的循证健康沟通的重要性。

Análise epidemiológica da mpox no século XXI: uma revisão narrativa Epidemiological analysis of mpox in the 21st century: a narrative review Análisis epidemiológico de la mpox en el siglo XXI: una revisión narrativa

Authors: Carlielma da Costa de Assunção, Pamela Ferreira Marques de Aquino, Samara Maria Cavalcante de Souza, Fabiolla da Silva dos Santos, Andréa Cristina Monteiro dos Santos, Luiz Henrique Campos Holanda, de Assunção Carlielma da Costa, dos Santos Fabiolla da Silva
Original abstract (English)
A mpox ganhou relevância internacional após a ampliação do número de casos observada a partir de 2022, modificando o cenário epidemiológico anteriormente concentrado principalmente em regiões da África Central e Ocidental. Este estudo teve como objetivo analisar a evolução temporal da doença, o perfil sociodemográfico e clínico dos casos, os padrões de transmissão e as principais medidas de vigilância e controle. Foi realizada uma revisão narrativa da literatura, de caráter descritivo e abordagem qualitativa, com buscas entre fevereiro e outubro de 2026 em bases científicas e documentos institucionais. Os estudos analisados evidenciaram expansão internacional da mpox e, no Brasil, predominância de notificações entre pessoas registradas como do sexo masculino ao nascimento no período avaliado. No Brasil, 91,8% dos 8.167 casos confirmados e prováveis notificados entre 7 de junho e 1o de outubro de 2022 eram de pessoas do sexo masculino ao nascimento. Entre os casos para os quais havia informação sobre exposição, o contato sexual foi o tipo de contato mais frequentemente relatado, sem excluir outras formas de contato próximo. Os achados reforçam a necessidade de vigilância epidemiológica contínua, diagnóstico laboratorial oportuno, rastreamento de contatos, vacinação conforme as recomendações vigentes e comunicação em saúde baseada em evidências e livre de estigmatização.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the mpox virus, examining its epidemiological evolution, sociodemographic and clinical profiles, transmission patterns, and surveillance and control measures. The central question is to understand the global spread of mpox and its characteristics in specific populations, particularly in Brazil.
Evidence base and method
The review is a narrative analysis of the literature, conducted between February and October 2026, using scientific databases and institutional documents. It is descriptive and qualitative in nature, without explicit mention of meta-analysis or pooled estimates.
Consensus and conclusions
The studies highlight the international expansion of mpox and note that in Brazil, the majority of confirmed and probable cases were reported among individuals assigned male sex at birth. Sexual contact was the most frequently reported mode of exposure, though other close contacts were also noted.
Controversies and gaps
The evidence does not report on specific controversies or evidence gaps related to the mpox virus. However, the review suggests a need for further research on transmission dynamics and the role of different populations in disease spread.
Research and practice implications
The findings emphasize the importance of continuous epidemiological surveillance, timely laboratory diagnosis, contact tracing, vaccination according to current guidelines, and evidence-based health communication that avoids stigmatization.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

撒哈拉以南非洲地区医护人员应对猴痘大流行的准备情况:快速回顾调查知识差距

Healthcare Workers’ Preparedness for Monkeypox Pandemic Response in SubSaharan Africa: A Rapid Review Investigating the Knowledge Gap
作者： Arole Darwin Touko, Ruth Tabi Amin, Gordon Takop Nchanji
摘要中文翻译背景:猴痘 是一种人畜共患病毒性疾病,由撒哈拉以南非洲 (SSA) 流行的 正痘病毒 引起,该病的死灰复燃凸显了改善卫生保健工作者 (HCW) 准备情况的迫切需要。尽管“同一个健康”方法具有潜力,但目前的实施仍然分散。本快速审查综合了 HCW 的知识、态度、信息来源以及 SSA 中 猴痘 准备障碍的证据,以确定培训需求和政策影响。方法:按照系统评价和荟萃分析的首选报告项目 (PRISMA) 指南,使用 MeSH 术语和布尔运算符对 2000 年至 2024 年间发表的研究进行 PubMed 和 ScienceDirect 搜索。纳入了评估 HCW 知识、态度或 SSA 中 猴痘 准备情况的研究,并提供英文或法文全文;那些涉及非医护人员人群、在 SSA 之外进行或缺乏主要数据的研究被排除在外。两名评审员使用标准化模板独立筛选和提取数据;使用 JBI 分析横断面研究批判性评估清单评估质量。结果:纳入了来自 388 份记录的五项研究(喀麦隆、埃塞俄比亚、尼日利亚、刚果民主共和国;n = 1,672 名医护人员)。只有 46.8% 的医护人员表现出足够的 猴痘 知识。障碍包括对社交媒体的依赖和获得正规培训的机会有限。对预防的积极态度与较高的知识水平相关。结论:尽管该地区的病死率接近全球平均水平的十倍,但只有不到一半的 SSA 医护人员表现出足够的 猴痘 知识;积极态度并未转化为临床准备,反映了严重的意向与行动差距;社交媒体作为信息来源占据主导地位,强调了在同一个健康培训框架内需要经过同行评审的替代方案。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
猴痘 是一种人畜共患病毒性疾病,由撒哈拉以南非洲 (SSA) 流行的 正痘病毒 引起,该病的死灰复燃凸显了卫生保健工作者 (HCW) 迫切需要加强做好准备。
证据基础与方法
遵循系统评价和荟萃分析的首选报告项目 (PRISMA) 指南,使用 MeSH 术语和布尔运算符对 2000 年至 2024 年间发表的研究进行 PubMed 和 ScienceDirect 搜索。纳入了评估 HCW 知识、态度或 SSA 中 猴痘 准备情况的研究,并提供英文或法文全文;那些涉及非医护人员人群、在 SSA 之外进行或缺乏主要数据的研究被排除在外。两名评审员使用标准化模板独立筛选和提取数据;使用 JBI 分析横断面研究批判性评估清单评估质量。
共识与结论
纳入了来自 388 份记录的五项研究(喀麦隆、埃塞俄比亚、尼日利亚、刚果民主共和国;n = 1,672 名医护人员)。只有 46.8% 的医护人员表现出足够的 猴痘 知识。障碍包括对社交媒体的依赖和获得正规培训的机会有限。对预防的积极态度与较高的知识水平相关。

尽管该地区的病死率接近全球平均水平的十倍,但只有不到一半的 SSA 医护人员表现出足够的 猴痘 知识;积极态度并未转化为临床准备,反映了严重的意向与行动差距;社交媒体作为信息来源占据主导地位,强调了在同一个健康培训框架内需要经过同行评审的替代方案。
科研与实践启示
本快速审查综合了 HCW 的知识、态度、信息来源以及 SSA 中 猴痘 准备障碍的证据,以确定培训需求和政策影响。

Healthcare Workers’ Preparedness for Monkeypox Pandemic Response in SubSaharan Africa: A Rapid Review Investigating the Knowledge Gap

Authors: Arole Darwin Touko, Ruth Tabi Amin, Gordon Takop Nchanji
Original abstract (English)
Background: Mpox is a zoonotic viral disease caused by an Orthopoxvirus endemic to sub-Saharan Africa (SSA), where its resurgence underscores the urgent need for improved preparedness among health care workers (HCWs). Despite the potential of a One Health approach, current implementations remain fragmented. This rapid review synthesises evidence on HCWs' knowledge, attitudes, information sources, and barriers to preparedness regarding Mpox in SSA to identify training needs and policy implications. Methods: Following Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic Reviews and Meta-Analyses (PRISMA) guidelines, PubMed and ScienceDirect were searched using MeSH terms and Boolean operators for studies published between 2000 and 2024. Studies assessing HCW knowledge, attitudes, or preparedness for Mpox in SSA with full-text availability in English or French were included; those involving non-HCW populations, conducted outside SSA, or lacking primary data were excluded. Two reviewers independently screened and extracted data using a standardised template; quality was assessed using the JBI Critical Appraisal Checklist for Analytical CrossSectional Studies. Results: Five studies from 388 records were included (Cameroon, Ethiopia, Nigeria, DRC; n = 1,672 HCWs). Only 46.8% of HCWs demonstrated adequate Mpox knowledge. Barriers included reliance on social media and limited access to formal training. Positive attitudes towards prevention correlated with higher knowledge levels. Conclusion: Fewer than half of SSA HCWs demonstrate adequate Mpox knowledge despite a regional case fatality rate nearly ten times the global average; positive attitudes are not translating into clinical preparedness, reflecting a critical intention-action gap; and social media dominates as an information source, underscoring the need for peer-reviewed alternatives within a One Health training framework.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Mpox is a zoonotic viral disease caused by an Orthopoxvirus endemic to sub-Saharan Africa (SSA), where its resurgence underscores the urgent need for improved preparedness among health care workers (HCWs).
Evidence base and method
Following Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic Reviews and Meta-Analyses (PRISMA) guidelines, PubMed and ScienceDirect were searched using MeSH terms and Boolean operators for studies published between 2000 and 2024. Studies assessing HCW knowledge, attitudes, or preparedness for Mpox in SSA with full-text availability in English or French were included; those involving non-HCW populations, conducted outside SSA, or lacking primary data were excluded. Two reviewers independently screened and extracted data using a standardised template; quality was assessed using the JBI Critical Appraisal Checklist for Analytical Cross-Sectional Studies.
Consensus and conclusions
Five studies from 388 records were included (Cameroon, Ethiopia, Nigeria, DRC; n = 1,672 HCWs). Only 46.8% of HCWs demonstrated adequate Mpox knowledge. Barriers included reliance on social media and limited access to formal training. Positive attitudes towards prevention correlated with higher knowledge levels.
Controversies and gaps
Fewer than half of SSA HCWs demonstrate adequate Mpox knowledge despite a regional case fatality rate nearly ten times the global average; positive attitudes are not translating into clinical preparedness, reflecting a critical intention-action gap; and social media dominates as an information source, underscoring the need for peer-reviewed alternatives within a One Health training framework.
Research and practice implications
This rapid review synthesises evidence on HCWs' knowledge, attitudes, information sources, and barriers to preparedness regarding Mpox in SSA to identify training needs and policy implications.

A 高优先级公共卫生新闻

全球猴痘:现状 - 疾病控制与预防中心 |疾病预防控制中心 (.gov)

Global Monkeypox: Current Situation - Centers for Disease Control and Prevention | CDC (.gov)
新闻简报CDC 2026 年 11 月 10 日的报告强调了全球正在发生的猴痘 暴发,自 2022 年以来主要由分支 IIb 驱动,在 120 多个国家/地区有超过 100,000 例病例。在美国,自 2024 年 11 月以来,至少 30 个州和波多黎各已报告了 190 多例实验室确诊病例,其中大多数病例与旅行或性接触有关。然而,自 2026 年 8 月以来,报告的 I 型分支病例中,最近没有国际旅行或与旅行者有已知联系的比例不断增加,这表明 Ib 型分支可能会过渡到持续的国内 传播。在美国,I 型猴痘造成的风险仍然很低,并且没有与这些病例相关的死亡病例。CDC 继续监测进化枝 I 和 II 暴发,为高危个体提供 疫苗接种 建议,并与公共卫生部门合作进行临床管理和预防。该机构还通过现有的 监测 系统加强早期检测,包括废水检测。
查看新闻五要素
时间
猴痘 暴发 自 2022 年以来一直在持续,截至 2026 年 11 月 10 日的最新数据表明,最近没有国际旅行或与旅行者有已知联系的 Ib 分支病例的比例不断增加。
地点与对象
美国至少有 30 个州和波多黎各报告了病例。大多数 I 类病例发生在同性恋、双性恋或其他男男性行为者中。
事件
CDC 报告了一种正在发生的全球猴痘 暴发,自 2022 年以来,分支 IIb 是主要类型。最近的 暴发 通过亲密或性接触传播,在美国没有与分支 I 病例相关的死亡。
规模、影响与风险
全球正在流行的 IIb 分支猴痘 暴发 已在 120 多个国家/地区造成超过 100,000 例病例。在美国,自 2024 年 11 月以来,已有 190 多例实验室确诊的 I 类病例,其中大多数病例与旅行或性接触有关。在美国,I 型猴痘造成的风险仍然很低,国内 传播 可能会转变为 Ib 型持续传播。
应对、状态与不确定性
CDC 继续监测美国的 I 型和 II 型猴痘病例,并正在进行 监测 测试,以识别发送到其痘病毒实验室的所有样本的进化型。CDC 为高危人群提供 疫苗接种 建议,并与公共卫生部门合作进行临床管理和预防。
A high priorityPublic-health news

Global Monkeypox: Current Situation - Centers for Disease Control and Prevention | CDC (.gov)

News Brief
The CDC's November 10, 2026, report highlights the ongoing global monkeypox outbreak, primarily driven by clade IIb since 2022, with over 100,000 cases in more than 120 countries. In the U.S., clade I monkeypox has been reported in over 190 laboratory-confirmed cases across at least 30 states and Puerto Rico since November 2024, with most cases linked to travel or sexual contact. However, since August 2026, an increasing proportion of clade I cases have been reported without recent international travel or known links to travelers, indicating a possible transition to sustained domestic transmission of clade Ib. The risk posed by clade I monkeypox in the U.S. remains low, and no deaths have been associated with these cases. CDC continues to monitor both clade I and II outbreaks, providing vaccination recommendations for at-risk individuals and collaborating with public health departments for clinical management and prevention. The agency also enhances early detection through existing surveillance systems, including wastewater testing.

What to know There are two types of the virus that causes monkeypox, clade I and clade II. Both types can be spread, treated, and prevented in the same ways. Each clade has two subclades (clade Ia and clade Ib; clade IIa and IIb). Recent outbreaks of monkeypox have spread initially through intimate or sexual contact. In some cases, monkeypox was then spread within households. Recent travel-associated clade I monkeypox cases have almost exclusively been attributed to subclade Ib; there have been no deaths associated with these cases and most people had relatively mild illness. The ongoing global outbreak of clade IIb monkeypox that began in 2022 has caused more than 100,000 cases in more than 120 total countries. CDC has vaccination recommendations for people at risk in the United States or who are traveling to countries with clade I outbreaks or new clade II outbreaks. Current situation In the United States Clade I monkeypox The risk posed by the clade I monkeypox outbreak to most people within the United States remains low. In the United States, since November 2024, there have been more than 190 laboratory-confirmed cases of clade I monkeypox in at least 30 states plus Puerto Rico. The majority of these cases are among gay, bisexual, or other men who have sex with men. Many of these cases: Had recently traveled from areas associated with the outbreak in Central and Eastern Africa; Reported sexual contact during travel to parts of Western Europe where newer outbreaks are being reported; OR Reported sexual contact with people who had traveled from these areas. Since August 2026, an increasing proportion of clade I cases in the United States have been reported in individuals with no recent international travel or known links to people who had recently traveled. Cases with presumed domestic transmission have been reported in 16 jurisdictions, indicating a likely transition to sustained clade Ib transmission in the United States CDC expects to see additional cases of both travel-related and domestically acquired cases of clade Ib occurring across the United States. CDC continues to monitor both clade II and clade I monkeypox cases across the United States and follow surveillance reports to monitor for any changes in demographics, disease severity, or case counts by jurisdiction. CDC also continues surveillance testing to identify monkeypox clade for all specimens sent to CDC's poxvirus laboratory. Spotlight Jurisdictions can contact poxvirus@cdc.gov for CDC technical support and clinical consultations for severe cases Monday-Friday, 8 a.m.-5 p.m. Clade II monkeypox Clade II monkeypox is still circulating at low levels. Fall 2025 marked the highest monthly case counts since the peak of the outbreak in 2022. Reminder Vaccination is an important tool in stopping the spread of monkeypox. If you have certain risk factors that make you eligible, you can help protect yourself from monkeypox by getting the 2-dose monkeypox vaccine. Monkeypox Vaccination Across the globe Clade I monkeypox Outbreaks of clade I monkeypox in Central and Eastern Africa continue. Beginning in Fall 2025, several countries in Western Europe began reporting clade Ib monkeypox cases among individuals who had no documented history of international travel. These cases were likely related to male-to-male sexual contact. Other travel-associated cases of clade I monkeypox have been reported from other parts of Africa, as well as Asia, Europe, the Middle East, North America, South America, and Australia. Recent monkeypox outbreaks have low death rates During recent outbreaks, death rates ranged from than less than 0.5% (clade Ib) to about 3% (clade Ia). Clade II is the type that caused the ongoing global outbreak that began in 2022. Fewer than 0.1% of people infected with clade II die from the disease. Clade II monkeypox For global case data for clade I and clade II monkeypox since January 1, 2024, see Global Mpox Trends. Confirmed cases include those that are laboratory confirmed as monkeypox virus and may include cases only confirmed as orthopoxvirus. These data are provided for situational awareness and are subject to change. Clade I Monkeypox Outbreaks Cases of clade I monkeypox have been diagnosed worldwide. Clade II Monkeypox Outbreaks Clade II monkeypox is spreading in the United States and around the world. What CDC is doing CDC raises awareness for U.S. healthcare providers, including the latest guidance for diagnosing monkeypox and clinical decision-making. CDC continues critical work to protect the United States from emerging and infectious diseases. CDC has collaborated closely with key partners in affected countries to help support efforts to stop monkeypox at the source. CDC works closely with U.S. state, tribal, local, and territorial public health departments to provide recommendations for clinical management, diagnosis, and prevention of domestic monkeypox cases. in the U.S. CDC continually increases capacity in communities across the United States for early detection of monkeypox through existing surveillance systems, including wastewater testing. CDC raises awareness for healthcare providers, including the latest guidance for diagnosing monkeypox and clinical decision-making. CDC collaborated with governmental and civil society partners in affected countries to collect and analyze case data, and to identify how monkeypox is spreading. CDC coordinated technical assistance in response to urgent in-country needs, including: increasing the number of monkeypox testing sites; improving specimen transport networks to quickly identify new cases; assisting with Ministry of Health-led vaccine implementation; strengthening emergency management systems; improving case surveillance; and training healthcare workers. CDC's staff throughout Africa provide critical, real-time information to inform U.S. preparedness efforts. In the United States CDC works closely with state, tribal, local, and territorial public health departments to provide recommendations for clinical management, diagnosis, and prevention of monkeypox cases in the U.S. CDC continually increases capacity in communities across the United States for early detection of monkeypox through existing surveillance systems, including wastewater testing. CDC raises awareness for healthcare providers, including the latest guidance for diagnosing monkeypox and clinical decision-making. CDC has information and recommendations for members of the public, including travelers: Destinations | Travelers' Health, Travel Health Notices, and Health Alert Network advisories. CDC continues to work with international partners to help reduce monkeypox transmission. Clade I Monkeypox Outbreaks - Centers for Disease Control and Prevention | CDC (.gov)
新闻简报CDC 报告自 2024 年 1 月以来,全球范围内持续存在 I 型猴痘 暴发,已有近 60,000 例确诊病例,150 多人死亡。截至 2026 年 10 月,美国至少 30 个州和波多黎各报告了 190 多例病例,其中大多数是男男性行为者。对于大多数美国人来说,这种风险仍然很低,但对于男男性行为者来说,这种风险是中等的。传播 动态发生了变化,美国涉及国内传播而不是国际旅行的病例比例不断增加。CDC 正在积极监测 暴发,预计欧洲和美国会出现更多病例。他们正在为预防、检测和病例管理提供指导,强调数据可能会发生变化并用于态势感知。高收入国家尚未报告 Ib 分支病例死亡,这些病例的病程相对较轻。

自 2024 年 1 月起,暴发 一直持续进行。截至 2026 年 10 月,正在对情况进行监测,自 2026 年 8 月以来报告的病例比例不断增加。

暴发 已在全球范围内得到报道,其中重点是美国,该国至少 30 个州以及波多黎各都有病例记录。在美国,大多数病例是同性恋、双性恋或其他男男性行为者。

A high priorityPublic-health news

Clade I Monkeypox Outbreaks - Centers for Disease Control and Prevention | CDC (.gov)

News Brief
The CDC has reported ongoing clade I monkeypox outbreaks globally since January 2024, with nearly 60,000 confirmed cases and over 150 deaths. In the United States, more than 190 cases have been reported in at least 30 states and Puerto Rico as of October 2026, with the majority among men who have sex with men. The risk to most people in the U.S. remains low, but it is moderate for men who have sex with men. Transmission dynamics have changed, with an increasing proportion of cases in the U.S. involving domestic spread rather than international travel. The CDC is actively monitoring the outbreak and expects more cases in Europe and the U.S. They are providing guidance for prevention, testing, and case management, emphasizing that the data are subject to change and for situational awareness. No deaths have been reported in clade Ib cases in high-income countries, and these cases are associated with relatively mild disease courses.

What to know Since January 2024, nearly 60,000 confirmed cases of clade I monkeypox have been reported globally, including more than 150 deaths. Newer outbreaks in several Western European countries are likely related to this ongoing outbreak. Transmission dynamics have changed. Only a small proportion of exported clade I monkeypox cases worldwide this year have originated in Central or Eastern African countries. Recent travel-associated clade I monkeypox cases have almost exclusively been attributed to subclade Ib; there have been no deaths associated with these cases and available data have detailed relatively mild disease courses. Outbreak overview In the United States The risk posed by the clade I monkeypox outbreak to most people within the United States remains low. The risk to men who have sex with men is low to moderate. Clade I cases in the United States have increased in frequency since March 2026. As of October 2026, there have been more than 190 clade I monkeypox cases reported in at least 30 states plus Puerto Rico. The majority of these cases are among gay, bisexual, or other men who have sex with men. Many of these cases: Had recently traveled from areas associated with the outbreak in Central and Eastern Africa; Reported sexual contact during travel to parts of Western Europe where newer outbreaks are being reported; OR Reported sexual contact with people who had traveled from these areas. Since August 2026, an increasing proportion of clade I cases in the United States have been reported in individuals with no recent international travel or known links to people who had recently traveled. Cases with presumed domestic transmission have been reported in at least 16 jurisdictions, indicating a likely transition to sustained clade Ib transmission in the United States. CDC expects to see additional cases of both travel-related and domestically acquired cases of clade Ib occurring across the United States. Preventing Monkeypox While Traveling Learn where monkeypox is spreading and how to lower your risk of getting it during travel. In Central and Eastern Africa Clade Ia monkeypox outbreaks began impacting western areas of the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC) in 2024. Available data indicate that clade Ia monkeypox has spread through sexual contact, regular household contact, and encounters with infected dead or live wild animals. Clade Ib monkeypox cases initially impacted eastern areas of DRC in late 2023. This subclade has driven much of the spread throughout Central and Eastern Africa and most travel-associated cases to other parts of the world. Available data indicate that these cases at first spread through heterosexual intimate or sexual contact between adults, then spread likely occurred within households, including to children. The disease spread in 2024 and 2025 to neighboring countries of DRC, only some of which are endemic for the virus that causes monkeypox, and sustained local spread occurred. Outside Central and Eastern Africa Several countries in other parts of Africa, Asia, Europe, North America, and South America, as well as Australia, have confirmed clade I monkeypox cases mostly in travelers who'd recently been in areas with clade I outbreaks. To date, there have been more than 300 clade Ib cases in high-income countries, and no deaths have been reported. Several countries in Western Europe are reporting clade Ib monkeypox cases among individuals who had no documented history of international travel. These cases were likely related to intimate or sexual exposure among men who have sex with men. We expect additional cases in Europe and the United States. For data since January 1, 2024, see 2022-24 Monkeypox (Monkeypox) Outbreak: Global Trends (shinyapps.io). Confirmed cases include those that are laboratory confirmed as monkeypox virus and may include cases only confirmed as orthopoxvirus. These data are provided for situational awareness and are subject to change. Clade I monkeypox and children Early in the Central and Eastern African outbreak that began in 2024, a high proportion of suspected cases in some areas were reported in children younger than 15 years of age. Historically, cases of monkeypox in children where monkeypox occurs regularly often happen after contact with wild animals that carry the disease. People then can spread the disease to close contacts, including in their households. The number of children with suspected monkeypox in this outbreak is probably due to spread within and between close households, the large number of younger people in affected countries, misdiagnosis of other diseases (such as measles), poor sanitary conditions, limited access to health services, and a high level of food insecurity and malnutrition. Based on what we know today, we don't expect to see the same sort of pattern in the United States for several reasons, including smaller household sizes, access to cleaning and disinfecting products, and improved access to medical care, equipment, and information about preventing spread while caring for patients. Notably, there are no consistent reports of schools as drivers of monkeypox transmission during any recent outbreaks. If cases of clade I monkeypox do happen in children the United States, CDC has clinical and public health guidance for reporting, testing, and effective case management. What CDC is doing CDC has been supporting DRC monkeypox research and response for more than 20 years. CDC and other U.S. government agencies have continued working in-country with partners in affected areas on disease surveillance, building local laboratory capacity, strengthening public health and laboratory workforce capacity, case investigation, case management, infection prevention and control, border health, and risk communication and community engagement. In the United States, CDC monitors both clade II and clade I monkeypox cases across the United States and follows surveillance reports to monitor for any changes in demographics, disease severity, or case counts by jurisdiction. CDC also performs surveillance testing to identify monkeypox clade for specimens sent to CDC's poxvirus laboratory. CDC staff brought laboratory supplies and trained local staff on laboratory equipment Resources Global Monkeypox: Current Situation There are outbreaks of clade I and clade II monkeypox around the world. Time
Since January 2024, the outbreak has been ongoing. As of October 2026, the situation is being monitored, with an increasing proportion of cases reported since August 2026.
Location and population
The outbreak has been reported globally, with a focus on the United States, where cases have been documented in at least 30 states plus Puerto Rico. The majority of cases in the U.S. are among gay, bisexual, or other men who have sex with men.
Event
Clade I monkeypox outbreaks have been reported globally, including in the United States. The CDC has confirmed these outbreaks and is monitoring their spread, particularly among men who have sex with men and through domestic transmission.
Scale, impact and risk
As of October 2026, more than 190 clade I monkeypox cases have been reported in the U.S. Globally, nearly 60,000 confirmed cases of clade I monkeypox have been reported, with over 150 deaths. The risk to most people in the U.S. remains low, but the risk to men who have sex with men is low to moderate. No deaths have been reported in clade Ib cases in high-income countries.
Response, status and uncertainty
The CDC is actively monitoring the outbreak, providing guidance for prevention, testing, and case management. The situation is ongoing, with expectations of additional cases in Europe and the U.S. The data are subject to change and provided for situational awareness.

猴痘 病例上升,促使县公共卫生服务范围扩大

Mpox cases rise, prompting expanded county public health outreach
新闻简报圣地亚哥县报告 猴痘 病例有所增加,自 2026 年 10 月以来已确诊 16 例,使全年总数达到 48 例。这一增长归因于该州内的旅行者,自 2025 年 10 月以来加利福尼亚州 I 类感染增加。该县已将病例公开报告频率提高到每周一次,并敦促有风险或可能接触过的个人接种两剂 JYNNEOS 疫苗。疫苗 可有效预防严重疾病、住院和死亡。由 正痘病毒 猴痘引起的 猴痘 自 2022 年以来一直在该地区流行,当年病例高峰达到 469 例。卫生官员正在开展外展活动,以应对病毒的传播,并确保通过地区公共卫生中心的性健康诊所获得 疫苗接种。
查看新闻五要素
时间
圣地亚哥县于 2026 年 11 月 13 日报告 猴痘 病例有所增加,自 2026 年 10 月以来已确诊 16 例。今年到目前为止,病例总数为 48 例,而 2025 年为 35 例。最近的增加据信来自该州境内的旅行者。
地点与对象
美国加利福尼亚州圣地亚哥县。受影响人群包括有 猴痘 风险的个人,特别是那些可能已暴露或未接种疫苗的人。
事件
圣地亚哥县卫生官员报告称,猴痘 病例有所增加,自 2026 年 10 月以来已确诊 16 例。该县已将病例公开报告从每月报告增加到每周报告。JYNNEOS 疫苗 建议那些有风险或认为自己可能已暴露的人使用。
规模、影响与风险
圣地亚哥县今年共确诊 48 例 猴痘 病例,自 2026 年 10 月以来报告了 16 例。其中 10 例发生在未接种疫苗的个体中。该疾病自 2022 年以来一直在该地区蔓延,当年病例数达到 469 例的峰值。疫苗 可有效预防严重疾病、住院和死亡。
应对、状态与不确定性
圣地亚哥县已将 猴痘 案件的公开报告频率提高到每周一次。卫生官员提醒有风险的人们尽快接种两剂 JYNNEOS 疫苗。疫苗 可在地区公共卫生中心的性健康诊所获得。该县正在开展外展活动,以解决病例增加的问题。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Mpox cases rise, prompting expanded county public health outreach

News Brief
San Diego County has reported an increase in mpox cases, with 16 confirmed since October 2026, bringing the total for the year to 48. This rise is attributed to travelers within the state, following an increase in Clade I infections in California since October 2025. The county has increased its public reporting of cases to weekly frequency and is urging individuals at risk or who may have been exposed to get the two-dose JYNNEOS vaccine. The vaccine is effective at preventing severe illness, hospitalization, and death. Mpox, caused by the Orthopoxvirus monkeypox, has been circulating in the region since 2022, with cases peaking at 469 that year. Health officials are conducting outreach to address the spread of the virus and ensure vaccination access through Sexual Health Clinics in regional Public Health Centers.

Mpox cases rise, prompting expanded county public health outreach San Diego County says 16 cases of mpox have been confirmed since October, bringing total to 48 so far this year Share this: Share on X (Opens in new window) X Share on Facebook (Opens in new window) Facebook Print (Opens in new window) Print Email a link to a friend (Opens in new window) Email Share on Reddit (Opens in new window) Reddit Minseung Chu, clinical assistant professor at UIC College of Nursing, prepares mpox vaccines at a vaccine event at Calor, a Latino nonprofit catering to people with HIV, in the Humboldt Park neighborhood of Chicago on Oct. 30, 2024. (Tess Crowley/Chicago Tribune) By City News Service | news@socalnews.com PUBLISHED: November 13, 2026 at 3:38 PM PST | UPDATED: November 15, 2026 at 5:12 AM PST Getting your Trinity Audio player ready... San Diego County on Friday reported an increase in local mpox cases with 16 confirmed since October, and 48 total so far this year, an increase from 35 reported in 2025.An infectious disease transmitted through close contact, including sexual contact, mpox has two variants that cause disease in people, Clade I and Clade II. According to the county, Clade II has driven most cases since the virus first appeared in the region in 2022. Clade I has circulated in California since 2025, and San Diego County health officials have confirmed 10 Clade I cases since July 2026.The symptoms of both variants are similar and the two-dose JYNNEOS vaccine protects against both types, a county statement read. Of the 16 cases reported since October, 10 occurred in unvaccinated people.California’s Department of Public Health first reported an increase in Clade I infections across the state in October 2025, tying it to returning travelers from Europe where Clade I cases were occurring.The more recent rise is believed to stem from those traveling within the state. As a result, San Diego County has increased its public reporting of cases from monthly to weekly.“With this recent increase in local cases, we are reminding people at risk to get the two-dose vaccine as soon as possible,” said Dr. Ankita Kadakia, county deputy public health officer. “It remains the best way to protect yourself and those around you. If you still need the second dose, it is never too late to receive it.”The disease has been spread in the San Diego region since 2022 when infections peaked at 469 cases. In years since, new cases have remained below 100 annually, the county reports.Symptoms include a rash, fever, swollen lymph nodes, body aches and fatigue. The rash or sores may be located anywhere on the body. This includes hands, feet, chest, face, mouth and genitals.“Although most cases are mild, the vaccine is very effective at preventing severe illness, hospitalization and death,” the county statement read.Anyone who may be at increased risk for mpox, or who believes they may have been exposed, should contact their healthcare provider and discuss vaccination options. San Diego County has JYNNEOS vaccines available at its Sexual Health Clinics located in regional Public Health Centers. B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

尼日利亚南部的孕产妇 猴痘 感染在分娩前常常未被识别 - Bioengineer.org

Maternal Mpox Infections in Southern Nigeria Often Go Unrecognized Before Delivery - Bioengineer.org
新闻简报2026 年 11 月 13 日发表在《BMC Infectious Medicines》上的一项研究强调了诊断尼日利亚南部孕产妇 猴痘 感染的挑战。这项研究于 2025 年 1 月至 5 月期间进行,研究了在三家医院接受治疗的三对母婴。没有一位母亲在怀孕或分娩期间被诊断出患有 猴痘,因为直到她们的男性新生儿在出生后第 4 天至第 9 天出现症状性疾病(包括发烧和广泛的水疱脓疱性皮肤病变)时,她们的感染才被发现。两名婴儿通过住院支持治疗完全康复,第三名婴儿于第 24 天在家中死亡,死因尚未确定。该研究强调需要改进诊断怀疑、获得分子检测以及持续的住院护理,以解决妊娠和新生儿期与 猴痘 相关的风险。在研究期间,抗病毒药物 特考韦瑞 无法在尼日利亚使用,限制了 治疗 的选择。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该研究于 2026 年 11 月 13 日发表,所描述的事件发生在 2025 年 1 月至 5 月期间。新生儿在出生后第 4 至 9 天出现症状,并在第 14 至 16 天期间寻求医院护理。
地点与对象
这项研究是在尼日利亚南部,特别是河流州进行的,涉及三名男性新生儿及其母亲。受影响人群包括孕妇及其新生儿。
事件
一项多中心回顾性病例系列发现,产妇 猴痘 感染通常在分娩前未被识别,新生儿出现症状,从而促使临床检测。该研究强调了流行地区母婴界面的诊断挑战。
规模、影响与风险
三名男性新生儿被诊断出患有 猴痘,其中两名完全康复,一名在家中死亡。没有一位母亲在怀孕或分娩期间被诊断出患有 猴痘,该研究强调需要改进一线诊断怀疑和分子检测,以降低感染母亲和新生儿的风险。
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究是对临床记录的回顾性审查,没有对死亡新生儿进行尸检,导致确切的死亡原因尚未得到证实。研究期间,特考韦瑞 在尼日利亚无法用于临床。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Maternal Mpox Infections in Southern Nigeria Often Go Unrecognized Before Delivery - Bioengineer.org

News Brief
A study published on November 13, 2026, in BMC Infectious Diseases highlights the challenges of diagnosing maternal mpox infections in southern Nigeria. The research, conducted between January and May 2025, examined three mother–infant pairs treated in three hospitals. None of the mothers were diagnosed with mpox during pregnancy or delivery, as their infections went unrecognized until their male neonates developed symptomatic illness, including fever and widespread vesiculopustular skin lesions, between days 4 and 9 of life. Two infants recovered fully with supportive inpatient therapy, while the third died at home on day 24 without a confirmed cause of death. The study underscores the need for improved diagnostic suspicion and access to molecular testing, as well as continuous inpatient care, to address risks associated with mpox during pregnancy and the neonatal period. The antiviral tecovirimat was unavailable for use in Nigeria during the study period, limiting treatment options.
Original source text (English)
HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER Monday, November 16, 2026 No Result View All Result Login HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER No Result View All Result No Result View All Result Home NEWS Science News Health Maternal Mpox Infections in Southern Nigeria Often Go Unrecognized Before Delivery by November 13, 2026 in Health Reading Time: 2 mins read 0 Maternal Mpox Infections in Southern Nigeria Often Go Unrecognized Before Delivery Share on FacebookShare on TwitterShare on LinkedinShare on RedditShare on Telegram A multicentre retrospective case series investigating maternal and neonatal mpox in southern Nigeria indicates that maternal infections frequently remain unrecognized prior to delivery, with infant presentations prompting clinical detection. Published in BMC Infectious Diseases, the study evaluated three mother–infant pairs treated across three hospitals between January and May 2025. In each documented instance, maternal illness went undetected until neonates developed symptomatic illness requiring medical attention, underscoring ongoing diagnostic hurdles at the maternal–neonatal interface in endemic regions. The study sought to address significant gaps in the characterization of mpox during pregnancy and the immediate neonatal period. Retrospective review of clinical records allowed investigators to examine maternal clinical histories, potential transmission pathways, clinical presentations, and ultimate patient outcomes. Cases were identified during routine hospital care when infants presented with characteristic vesiculopustular illness. The clinical cohort comprised three male neonates whose diagnoses were laboratory-confirmed using real-time polymerase chain reaction testing performed on lesion swabs. Discover more infectious diseases infection Infections infections Check Symptoms Clinical histories revealed that two of the mothers developed fever and vesiculopustular eruptions during their third trimester of pregnancy. The third mother experienced a brief febrile episode without an apparent or documented rash approximately one day prior to delivery. None of the mothers were clinically suspected of having mpox before giving birth, and none underwent diagnostic molecular testing for monkeypox virus infection during their pregnancies or deliveries. All three male infants subsequently exhibited fever and widespread vesiculopustular skin lesions emerging between days 4 and 9 of life, eventually presenting to clinical facilities between days 14 and 16. While clinicians considered administering the antiviral agent tecovirimat, the drug was unavailable for clinical use within Nigeria during the active study period. Clinical trajectories diverged among the three hospitalized neonates. Two infants achieved full recovery following supportive inpatient therapy. Conversely, the third neonate died at home on day 24 of life after an interruption of hospital care. Because no post-mortem examination was performed, the precise immediate cause of death could not be verified by medical teams. Discover more Study Biology Read Med Journals Shop Baby Gear Track Pregnancy Buy Maps The documented presentations demonstrate the substantial clinical and operational challenges surrounding mpox management at the maternal–newborn interface across endemic settings. When maternal infections go unconfirmed, clinical investigation depends almost entirely on the emergence of early, visible manifestations in newborns. Sustaining continuous inpatient management represents another critical priority identified by the investigators. The authors conclude that improving frontline diagnostic suspicion, expanding access to timely molecular testing, and ensuring unbroken continuity of care will be vital to mitigating risks for infected mothers and neonates. Time
The study was published on November 13, 2026, and the events described occurred between January and May 2025. Neonates presented with symptoms between days 4 and 9 of life, and hospital care was sought between days 14 and 16.
Location and population
The study was conducted in southern Nigeria, specifically in Rivers State, involving three male neonates and their mothers. The affected population includes pregnant women and their newborns.
Event
A multicentre retrospective case series found that maternal mpox infections often went unrecognized before delivery, with neonates presenting with symptomatic illness that prompted clinical detection. The study highlights diagnostic challenges at the maternal–neonatal interface in endemic regions.
Scale, impact and risk
Three male neonates were diagnosed with mpox, with two recovering fully and one dying at home. None of the mothers were diagnosed with mpox during pregnancy or delivery, and the study emphasizes the need for improved frontline diagnostic suspicion and molecular testing to mitigate risks for infected mothers and neonates.
Response, status and uncertainty
The study was a retrospective review of clinical records, and no post-mortem examination was performed on the deceased neonate, leaving the precise cause of death unverified. The antiviral tecovirimat was unavailable for clinical use in Nigeria during the study period.
