卫生官员警告称,圣地亚哥县 猴痘 病例呈上升趋势 - 补丁

Mpox Cases On The Rise In San Diego County, Health Officials Warn - Patch
新闻简报圣地亚哥县卫生官员报告称,猴痘 病例有所增加,自 2026 年 10 月以来已确诊 16 例,今年报告了 48 例。这种增加促使扩大了外展工作并重新开展了 疫苗接种 活动,因为 JYNNEOS 疫苗 被强调为预防严重疾病、住院和死亡的最佳方法。在最近的病例中,有 10 例涉及未接种疫苗的个体,这凸显了 疫苗接种 的重要性。该县已将报告频率提高到每周一次,并鼓励那些处于危险中或接触过的人寻求医疗建议。疫苗 可通过县性健康诊所获得,包括未参保和参保不足的居民。最近的病例激增是在 2022 年达到峰值 469 例之后,此后每年病例数一直保持在 100 例以下。官员们强调需要及时注射 疫苗接种,并指出尚未接受第二剂疫苗的人仍然可以获得第二剂疫苗。
查看新闻五要素
时间
自 2026 年 10 月以来,圣地亚哥县报告 猴痘 病例有所增加,此后已确诊 16 例,今年迄今已报告 48 例。自最近增加以来,该县已将报告频率提高到每周一次。
地点与对象
美国加利福尼亚州圣地亚哥县。受影响的人群包括接触过该病毒的个人以及风险较高的人,特别是通过密切的身体接触(包括性接触)的人。
事件
圣地亚哥县卫生官员已证实 猴痘 感染有所增加,自 2026 年 10 月以来报告了 16 例病例,今年报告了 48 例。他们正在扩大外展工作并鼓励 疫苗接种,特别是针对未接种疫苗的个人。
规模、影响与风险
该县2025年全年报告了35起猴痘病例,今年这一数字已增加至48起。自 10 月份以来的 16 例病例中,有 10 例涉及未接种疫苗的人。建议使用 JYNNEOS 疫苗 来预防严重疾病、住院和死亡。
应对、状态与不确定性
卫生官员正在加强监测、扩大外展范围并推广 疫苗接种。他们已开始每周进行病例报告,并鼓励那些有风险或接触过的人联系医疗保健提供者。该县还通过性健康诊所为未参保和参保不足的居民提供 疫苗。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Mpox Cases On The Rise In San Diego County, Health Officials Warn - Patch

News Brief
San Diego County health officials have reported a rise in mpox cases, with 16 confirmed since October 2026 and 48 cases reported this year. This increase has prompted expanded outreach efforts and renewed vaccination campaigns, as the JYNNEOS vaccine is highlighted as the best way to prevent severe illness, hospitalization, and death. Of the recent cases, 10 involved unvaccinated individuals, underscoring the importance of vaccination. The county has increased reporting frequency to weekly and is encouraging those at risk or exposed to seek medical advice. Vaccines are available through county sexual health clinics, including for uninsured and underinsured residents. The recent surge follows a peak of 469 cases in 2022, with annual cases remaining below 100 since then. Officials emphasize the need for prompt vaccination, noting that the second dose is still available for those who have not yet received it.
Original source text (English)
Health & Fitness Mpox Cases On The Rise In San Diego County, Health Officials Warn Officials say evidence of local transmission has prompted increased monitoring and renewed vaccination efforts. SAN DIEGO, CA — San Diego County health officials are expanding outreach efforts amid a rise in mpox infections, with 16 cases confirmed since October and 48 reported so far this year. The county reported 35 cases in all of 2025. Mpox spreads through close physical contact, including sexual contact. Two types of the virus, Clade I and Clade II, cause illness in humans. Clade II has accounted for most local cases since mpox emerged in the region in 2022. Find out what's happening in San Diegofor free with the latest updates from Patch. Clade I has circulated in California since 2025. San Diego County health officials have confirme 10 cases of Clade I mpox since July, with evidence suggesting the virus is spreading locally. Of the 16 cases reported since October, 10 involved people who were not vaccinated, according to health officials. Find out what's happening in San Diegofor free with the latest updates from Patch. "With this recent increase in local cases, we are reminding people at risk to get the two-dose vaccine as soon as possible," said Dr. Ankita Kadakia, the county's deputy public health officer. "It remains the best way to protect yourself and those around you. If you still need the second dose, it is never too late to receive it." Symptoms include a rash, fever, swollen lymph nodes, body aches and fatigue. The rash or sores can appear anywhere on the body, including hands, feet, chest, face, mouth and genitals. Although most infections are mild, the two-dose JYNNEOS vaccine protects against both types of mpox and helps prevent severe illness, hospitalization and death, officials said. Mpox cases in San Diego County peaked at 469 in 2022 and have remained below 100 annually since then. In response to the recent increase, the county has begun reporting mpox cases weekly instead of monthly. Health officials encourage people at increased risk or those who may have been exposed to contact their health care provider about vaccination. Vaccines are available through county sexual health clinics, including for uninsured and underinsured residents. For more information, visit the county's mpox webpage. Residents can also sign up for updates by texting COSD MPOX to 98256. Get more local news delivered straight to your inbox. Sign up for free Patch newsletters and alerts

巴西 2026 年首例 猴痘 死亡:里约病例上升 - Daily Beirut

Brazil First Mpox Death 2026: Cases Rise in Rio - Daily Beirut
新闻简报Brazil has confirmed its first mpox death of 2026, with health authorities monitoring a rise in infections that remains below historical peak levels. The fatal case occurred in Rio de Janeiro, where confirmed infections between September and November doubled compared to the rest of the year. The Fiocruz Institute, which operates under Brazil’s Ministry of Health, reported 1,009 confirmed viral infections this year. While case numbers have increased, Brazilian health authorities do not classify the current situation as an outbreak. In response, the government strengthened surveillance protocols and purchased 25,000 additional doses of the mpox vaccine. Officials note that infection rates remain significantly lower than the historic peak observed in 2022. The outbreak is concentrated in São Paulo and Rio de Janeiro, two of the nation’s three most populous states, within a total population of 214 million. Mpox is caused by a virus in the poxvirus family, with symptoms including fever, muscle pain, and large skin lesions resembling boils, and transmission occurs from animals to humans or between people through close physical contact. The World Health Organization declared mpox a public health emergency of international concern in both 2022 and 2024 due to rapid spread in certain countries.
查看新闻五要素
时间
2026 年 11 月 16 日星期一(活动日期);2026 年 11 月 15 日(发布日期)。
地点与对象
巴西,特别是里约热内卢,病例集中在圣保罗和里约热内卢,这两个是该国人口最多的三个州中的两个,总人口为214百万。
事件
巴西于 2026 年确认了首例 猴痘 死亡病例,卫生当局监测到感染人数上升,但仍低于历史峰值水平。这起致命病例发生在里约热内卢,该州 9 月至 11 月期间确诊感染病例比今年其余时间翻了一番。
规模、影响与风险
The Fiocruz Institute reported 1,009 confirmed viral infections this year.感染率仍显着低于 2022 年观察到的历史峰值。目前的情况未被巴西卫生当局归类为 暴发。
应对、状态与不确定性
为了应对这一趋势,政府加强了 监测 方案,并额外购买了 25,000 剂 猴痘 疫苗。

Mpox case rise in SD County, prompting expanded county public health outreach
新闻简报圣地亚哥县报告 猴痘 病例有所增加,自 2026 年 10 月以来已确诊 16 例,2026 年迄今确诊病例总数为 48 例,高于 2025 年的 35 例。这一上升归因于当地的 猴痘 病例,大多数病例与未接种疫苗的个人有关。该县扩大了公共卫生范围,并将报告频率提高到每周一次。卫生官员强调,JYNNEOS 疫苗 可预防 Clade I 和 Clade II 猴痘 变种,在预防严重疾病、住院和死亡方面非常有效。他们敦促高危人群联系医疗保健提供者以获取 疫苗接种 选项。该县的应对措施包括在地区公共卫生中心的性健康诊所提供 疫苗。此更新反映了该地区为管理 猴痘 正在进行的公共卫生工作。

Mpox case rise in SD County, prompting expanded county public health outreach

News Brief
San Diego County has reported an increase in mpox cases, with 16 confirmed since October 2026 and 48 total so far in 2026, up from 35 in 2025. The rise is attributed to local transmission, with most cases linked to unvaccinated individuals. The county has expanded its public health outreach and increased reporting frequency to weekly. Health officials emphasize that the JYNNEOS vaccine, which protects against both Clade I and Clade II mpox variants, is highly effective at preventing severe illness, hospitalization, and death. They urge at-risk individuals to contact healthcare providers for vaccination options. The county's response includes making vaccines available at Sexual Health Clinics in regional Public Health Centers. This update reflects the ongoing public health efforts to manage mpox in the region.

Newsletters/Alerts Skip to Content News Weather Sports KUNA Health Life Shop Play Share Palm Springs 74° News Mpox case rise in SD County, prompting expanded county public health outreach Cropped NIAID / CC BY 2.0 By City News Service Published November 13, 2026 11:34 PM SAN DIEGO, Calif. (KESQ) - San Diego County today reported an increase in local mpox cases with 16 confirmed since October 2026, and 48 total so far this year, an increase from 35 reported in 2025. An infectious disease transmitted through close contact, including sexual contact, mpox has two variants that cause disease in people, Clade I and Clade II. According to the county, Clade II has driven most cases since the virus first appeared in the region in 2022. Clade I has circulated in California since 2025, and San Diego County health officials have confirmed 10 Clade I cases since July 2026. The symptoms of both variants are similar and the two-dose JYNNEOS vaccine protects against both types, a county statement read. Of the 16 cases reported since October 2026, 10 occurred in unvaccinated people. California's Department of Public Health first reported an increase in Clade I infections across the state in October 2025, tying it to returning travelers from Europe where Clade I cases were occurring. The more recent rise is believed to stem from those traveling within the state. As a result, San Diego County has increased its public reporting of cases from monthly to weekly. "With this recent increase in local cases, we are reminding people at risk to get the two-dose vaccine as soon as possible,'' said Dr. Ankita Kadakia, county deputy public health officer. "It remains the best way to protect yourself and those around you. If you still need the second dose, it is never too late to receive it." The disease has been spread in the San Diego region since 2022 when infections peaked at 469 cases. In years since, new cases have remained below 100 annually, the county reports. Cases in Riverside County remain low, with 16 reported all year. Symptoms include a rash, fever, swollen lymph nodes, body aches and fatigue. The rash or sores may be located anywhere on the body. This includes hands, feet, chest, face, mouth and genitals. "Although most cases are mild, the vaccine is very effective at preventing severe illness, hospitalization and death,'' the county statement read. Anyone who may be at increased risk for mpox, or who believes they may have been exposed, should contact their healthcare provider and discuss vaccination options. San Diego County has JYNNEOS vaccines available at its Sexual Health Clinics located in regional Public Health Centers. Event

Scale, impact and risk
There were 48 total mpox cases in 2026, with 16 reported since October 2026. Of these, 10 occurred in unvaccinated individuals. The county emphasizes that the vaccine is effective at preventing severe illness, hospitalization, and death.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

纽约市 猴痘 病例增加

New York City sees increase in mpox cases
新闻简报纽约市的 猴痘 病例有所增加,特别是在 2026 年夏末。根据纽约市健康和心理卫生局的数据,这种上升归因于季节性模式,9 月份的周高点为 19 例。自那以后,七周中有五周至少有八例病例,超过了三月底至九月的峰值。然而,官员们指出,截至 2026 年 11 月 13 日,病例数呈下降趋势。暴发 主要影响男男性行为者,纽约州其他地区的病例数也略有增加。卫生官员建议高危人群(包括前往欧洲旅行的人)使用 疫苗接种,并强调完成 JYNNEOS 系列的重要性。该市于 2026 年 3 月报告了首例 Clade I 猴痘 病例,该病例与美国流传的 Clade II 变种不同。该回应基于卫生部门网站的数据,情况仍在调查中。
查看新闻五要素
时间
纽约市 猴痘 病例的增加始于 2026 年 8 月,9 月份达到 19 例的周高点,并在整个夏末持续增加病例数,自 2026 年 9 月 19 日以来,七周中有 5 周至少有 8 例。最新数据显示,截至 2026 年 11 月 13 日发布之日,病例数目前呈下降趋势。
地点与对象
纽约市,特别是五个行政区,以及更广泛的纽约州。猴痘 暴发 主要影响男男性行为者,其中一些病例涉及旅行归来的人。
事件
纽约市的 猴痘 病例有所增加,特别是在 2026 年夏末。纽约市健康和心理卫生局报告了这一上升趋势,并将其归因于季节性增加,同时还指出,截至 2026 年 11 月 13 日,病例数一直呈下降趋势。
规模、影响与风险
2026 年 9 月至 10 月期间,五个行政区报告了 41 例病例。该市 9 月份出现了 19 例病例,创下每周新高,自 9 月 19 日以来,七周中有五周至少有 8 例。这种风险被描述为季节性风险,卫生官员表示,病例目前正在下降。
应对、状态与不确定性
市卫生官员将病例上升归因于季节性增加,并表示病例目前呈下降趋势。他们向高危人群(包括前往欧洲旅行的人群)推荐 疫苗接种,并强调完成 JYNNEOS 系列的重要性。该回应基于卫生部网站的数据。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

New York City sees increase in mpox cases

News Brief
New York City has seen an increase in mpox cases, particularly in the latter part of the summer of 2026. According to the New York City Department of Health and Mental Hygiene, the uptick is attributed to seasonal patterns, with a weekly high of 19 cases in September. Since then, five out of seven weeks have had at least eight cases, surpassing the previous peak from late March to September. However, officials note that cases are now trending downward as of November 13, 2026. The outbreak has primarily affected men who have sex with men, and there has been a slight increase in cases in other parts of New York State as well. Health officials recommend vaccination for at-risk individuals, including those traveling to Europe, and emphasize the importance of completing the JYNNEOS series. The city reported its first case of Clade I mpox in March 2026, which is different from the Clade II variant that has been circulating in the US. The response is based on data from the Health Department website, and the situation remains under investigation.
Original source text (English)

Log In Register Search: Search: News All Arts Crime Politics Perspectives Things to Do Local Events Post an Event Business Events Games Jobs Contact Us Digital Editions Webinars Podcasts Health New York City sees increase in mpox cases By Matt Tracy ... Comments Posted on November 13, 2026 The New York City Department of Health and Mental Hygiene’s offices in Long Island City, Queens. Matt Tracy After a mostly quiet summer, mpox cases started to increase in New York City in August and there have been dozens of cases per month since then, according to the New York City Department of Health and Mental Hygiene. The latest numbers show the lingering impact of mpox, which has overwhelmingly impacted men who have sex with men since a 2022 outbreak hit the US. The city saw as many as 100 cases per day at one point that year as activists pushed the Biden administration for more doses of the Jynneos vaccine, which protects against mpox. Following the initial outbreak, mpox cases dipped, but they nonetheless continued to circulate in New York City, where cases have gone up and down over the course of several years. Early last year, the city was seeing very few cases — sometimes none or only one or two in a given week — but cases started increasing in April of 2025 and gradually rose until hitting 23 in one week in early October of last year. From there, cases decreased over the course of several months, and there were only a handful of cases from the spring of 2026 until the late summer. However, cases started to creep up in the latter part of this summer: The week of Aug. 29 saw six cases — the most since March — and subsequent weeks continued to see more cases, eventually hitting 13 in one week in the middle of September. There were 41 cases reported across the five boroughs between Sept. 6 and Oct. 3. After reaching a weekly high of 19 cases on Sept. 19, the city has still seen elevated cases: Since then, five out of seven weeks have had at least eight cases — more than the city ever saw in a week from late March until September. City health officials are attributing the uptick to “seasonal increases” and insisted that cases are now declining, even as cases have increased in consecutive weeks. “Mpox cases follow a seasonal pattern that typically decrease after summer, with a late-summer peak this year,” the New York City Department of Health and Mental Hygiene told Gay City News in a written statement. “Data recently posted to the Health Department website shows cases are now trending downward. While we are not noticing a unique increase in cases compared to the previous year, we have seen recent cases of clade I mpox including among people returning from travel.” Meanwhile, there has also been a slight increase in mpox cases in other parts of New York State. According to the New York State Department of Health’s mpox data, which shows cases outside of New York City, the rest of the Empire State saw either no cases or one case per month from March until August, but there were six cases in September and 19 cases in October. According to health officials, the variant of mpox that has circulated in New York and the US is Clade II. A different kind of mpox, clade I, has been associated with outbreaks in the Democratic Republic of the Congo and other countries in Central and Eastern Africa. In March of this year, New York City reported its first case of Clade I. “New Yorkers at risk for mpox, including those who may be traveling to Europe, should still get vaccinated,” the New York City Department of Health and Mental Hygiene said. “The NYC Health Department recommends starting and completing the two-dose JYNNEOS series. If you’ve already received the first dose, it is never too late to get the second. To find a location near you, please visit nyc.gov/healthmap or a Health Department Sexual Health Clinic for low-to-no cost vaccination.” Conversations Profile All comments are subject to our Community Guidelines. Schneps Media does not endorse the views shared by readers in our comment sections. The response is based on data from the Health Department website.

尽管存在表面污染问题,但公共厕所 猴痘 风险较低 - 泰国 PBS

Public toilet mpox risk low despite surface contamination concerns - Thai PBS
新闻简报泰国 PBS verify 于 2026 年 11 月 10 日报道,尽管担心表面污染,但从公共马桶座圈感染 猴痘 的风险非常低。文章强调,虽然病毒可以在马桶和水龙头等表面发现,但除非皮肤上有割伤或伤口,否则不太可能通过公共卫生间传播 传播。泰国疾病控制部的 Suchada Jiamsiri 和国家基因工程和生物技术中心的 Anan Jongkaewwattana 博士等专家强调,通过马桶座圈感染的可能性很小。他们建议使用酒精消毒剂,并在使用前检查清洁度。2026年11月9日的监测数据显示,之前占优势的Clade IIb菌株已经下降,而Clade Ib已成为泰国的优势菌株。文章还指出,猴痘 可以通过直接身体接触传播,而不仅仅是性接触,并建议个人避免开放性伤口并确保适当的卫生习惯。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该文章发表于2026年11月10日,猴痘 监测数据于2026年11月9日发布。风险评估和建议基于当前的科学理解和专家意见。
地点与对象
该分析重点关注泰国的公共卫生间,特别是使用这些设施的公众。还提到了泰国的猴痘情况,特别提到了该国的优势菌株。
事件
泰国 PBS verify 报道称,尽管存在表面污染问题,但从公共马桶座圈感染 猴痘 的风险非常低。这是基于专家意见和关于表面污染的科学研究。
规模、影响与风险
猴痘病毒 已在马桶和水龙头等表面上检测到,但通过公共马桶座圈的 传播 被认为极不可能,除非皮肤上有割伤或伤口。自 2026 年中期以来,泰国的优势菌株已转移至 Clade Ib。
应对、状态与不确定性
泰国 PBS verify 采访了专家并审查了科学研究。风险评估基于现有知识,文章提供了公共卫生间卫生习惯的建议。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Public toilet mpox risk low despite surface contamination concerns - Thai PBS

News Brief
Thai PBS Verify reported on November 10, 2026, that the risk of contracting mpox from a public toilet seat is very low, despite concerns about surface contamination. The article highlights that while the virus can be found on surfaces like toilet bowls and taps, transmission through public restrooms is unlikely unless there are cuts or wounds on the skin. Experts, including Suchada Jiamsiri from Thailand’s Department of Disease Control and Dr. Anan Jongkaewwattana from the National Center for Genetic Engineering and Biotechnology, emphasized that the likelihood of infection from a toilet seat is minimal. They recommended using alcohol-based disinfectants and checking for cleanliness before use. Surveillance data from November 9, 2026, showed that the previously dominant Clade IIb strain has declined, while Clade Ib has become the dominant strain in Thailand. The article also noted that mpox can spread through direct physical contact, not just sexual contact, and advised individuals to avoid open wounds and ensure proper hygiene practices.
Original source text (English)
เว็บในเครือ เว็บไซต์ในเครือ ALTV ทีวีเรียนสนุก VIPA ทุกความสุข...ดูฟรี ไม่มีโฆษณา The Active พื้นที่นําเสนอวาระของสังคม Thai PBS Kids เรื่องราวดี ๆ สําหรับครอบครัว Thai PBS Podcast View The World via The Voice Thai PBS World We Bring Thailand to The World เว็บไซต์บริการ C-SITE เพราะพลังการสื่อสารอยู่ในมือคุณ Locals นิเวศสื่อสาธารณะท้องถิ่นคุณภาพ Policy Watch จับตาอนาคตประเทศไทย The Visual Making Data Visible Thai PBS Verify ตรวจสอบข่าวปลอม คัดกรองข่าวจริง หน้าหลัก ข่าว รายการ NOW ชมสด ชมสด ชมย้อนหลัง ผังรายการ บริการดิจิตอล English องค์กร สมัครงาน/ฝึกงาน ข่าวประชาสัมพันธ์ คณะกรรมการนโยบาย ส.ส.ท. สภาผู้ชมและผู้ฟังรายการ รับเรื่องร้องเรียน ติดต่อเรา About Thai PBS ค้นหา ☰ หน้าหลัก ข่าว รายการ NOW ชมสด ชมย้อนหลัง ผังรายการ บริการดิจิตอล Home Categories Politics Around the world Science & Tech Social Environment & Disaster Health Issues Thai Flood Middle East Crisis Thai Cambodia Situation 2026 Thai Election AI Slops Impersonation Scammers Article Editor's Talk Analysis Interview How To News & Activities News PR News Trainings & Workshops Networking & Partnership Awards & Recognition About Us History Our Funding Methodology Corrections And Updates Suggest a Fact Check Verify Statistics FAQ Our Staff AAATH Public toilet mpox risk low despite surface contamination concerns TruthNov 10, 202617:54|Social & Health Sitting on a toilet seat in a public restroom carries only a very low risk of contracting mpox, despite online claims warning people to avoid public toilets because of the disease. Thai PBS Verify found that although the virus may contaminate surfaces, experts say transmission through toilet seats is unlikely, particularly for people without cuts or open wounds on the skin. However, people should avoid contact with mucous membranes and wash their hands thoroughly after using public facilities. Contents Thai PBS Verify found this information on Facebook Can you really catch mpox from a public toilet seat? Mpox situation in Thailand Symptoms to watch for How is mpox transmitted? Verification Process Back to top Thai PBS Verify found this information on Facebook A Facebook page shared an image carrying the message: “Mpox and public toilet seats. Are you at risk? The clear answer on whether you can really catch mpox.” The post stated: “Mpox and public toilet seats. Are you at risk? The clear answer on whether you can really catch mpox.” It also included the warning: “Urgent warning!! Be careful when using toilet seats in public restrooms because mpox is currently spreading. If possible, avoid shared toilets altogether because this disease is very dangerous. #AvoidPublicToiletsIfPossible” The post was published on Nov 8, 2026. Can you really catch mpox from a public toilet seat? Suchada Jiamsiri, director of the AIDS and Sexually Transmitted Infections Division, Department of Disease Control Suchada Jiamsiri, director of the AIDS and Sexually Transmitted Infections Division at Thailand’s Department of Disease Control, told Thai PBS Verify that scientific studies on surface contamination are typically conducted in homes, accommodation or environments where infected individuals have stayed for some time. The virus can be detected on various surfaces, including toilet bowls and taps. “In the case of public restrooms, we do not know whether a person with mpox has actually used the facility. Therefore, portraying public toilets as a particularly dangerous source of infection may cause unnecessary alarm. Risks should be assessed according to the specific setting and the likelihood of exposure to an infected individual,” she said. For the general public, Ms Suchada said there was little reason for concern. “There is no need to be overly worried because patients are not all sharing the same public restroom. From a disease prevention and control perspective, this is not considered a major concern. It is different from toilets within a patient’s home, which should be thoroughly cleaned after recovery,” she said. Although the virus may be detected on surfaces, touching a contaminated surface does not automatically result in infection, particularly when the skin is intact. The likelihood of infection through surfaces in an ordinary public restroom is extremely low because viable virus would need to remain present, such as fresh fluid from a lesion contaminating a toilet seat, a scenario experts say is unlikely in most public facilities. For those concerned about hygiene, she recommended using alcohol-based disinfectants to wipe hard surfaces such as toilet seats and flush buttons. The mpox virus is relatively easy to inactivate with alcohol. For hand hygiene, washing with soap and water is considered sufficient. She added that how long the virus survives on surfaces depends on environmental conditions including sunlight, temperature, humidity and whether the surface remains dry. While survival times vary, wiping surfaces with alcohol may provide reassurance. Nevertheless, she said public restrooms are generally not something that should cause significant concern. Dr Anan Jongkaewwattana, director of the Innovative Animal Health and Management Research Group, National Center for Genetic Engineering and Biotechnology Thai PBS Verify also interviewed Dr Anan Jongkaewwattana, who said that if a person with mpox used a toilet and lesions came into contact with the seat, viral contamination could occur. However, the next user would face only a very small risk of infection if their skin remained intact. “The skin on the buttocks and thighs is relatively thick and consists of multiple layers. It is not a mucous membrane or delicate tissue through which the virus can easily enter the body. However, if there are scratches or open wounds, infection becomes more possible,” he said. He added that infection could theoretically occur if someone touched a contaminated toilet seat and then touched their eyes, nose or mouth, areas where the virus can enter more easily through mucous membranes. Laboratory tests have detected viral material on surfaces for several hours using PCR methods. However, there is currently no evidence confirming that virus detected two hours later remains as infectious as virus freshly shed from a patient. Exposure to air, humidity and environmental conditions may reduce infectivity. Overall, he said transmission from a public toilet seat is highly unlikely unless the exposed skin has cuts or wounds. Emeritus Professor Amorn Leelarasamee, Thai infectious disease and internal medicine specialist Thai PBS Verify also consulted Emeritus Professor Amorn Leelarasamee, an infectious disease specialist, who noted that mpox cases are increasing in Thailand. He stressed that the disease is not transmitted solely through sexual contact but can also spread through direct physical contact, including contact with lesions on the buttocks, hands or mouth. “If it is necessary to use a public restroom, check that it is clean before sitting down. If alcohol spray is available, disinfect the seat first or use a toilet-seat cover. People should also ensure there are no open wounds on the buttocks or hands. Any cuts should be covered with a plaster. After using the restroom, wash thoroughly with soap and water and use alcohol gel to clean your hands to reduce the risk of infection,” he said. He explained that the virus has difficulty penetrating healthy skin. However, women should take particular care regarding the vaginal area, while men should be mindful of the urethra and anal area, especially if there are wounds or exposed mucous membranes that could provide an entry point for infection. He added that mpox is spread primarily through contact, making personal hygiene and awareness of symptoms important when using public facilities. Mpox situation in Thailand According to Thailand’s Department of Disease Control, mpox continues to circulate in the country. As of Oct 31, 2026, Thailand had recorded 470 cumulative cases and three deaths since the start of the year, according to a report issued on Nov 3, 2026. Surveillance data released on Nov 9, 2026, showed that the previously dominant Clade IIb strain, which drove the outbreak from 2022 onwards, has gradually declined in proportion. Meanwhile, Clade Ib has increased since mid-2026 and has become the dominant strain in Thailand. The Department of Disease Control said the Clade Ib strain has a fatality rate of less than 0.5%. However, infected patients can still develop severe symptoms or complications, particularly those with weakened immune systems or underlying medical conditions. Symptoms to watch for Information published by the Department of Disease Control on Nov 9, 2026, noted that mpox symptoms can vary and may not initially present with obvious skin lesions. Symptoms may include: Fever, headache and muscle aches Swollen lymph nodes Sore throat or mouth ulcers Rashes, blisters or pus-filled lesions Lesions around the genital or anal area Anal pain or inflammation of mucous membranes Some patients may experience symptoms resembling tonsillitis or abscesses around the buttocks before developing visible rashes or lesions. How is mpox transmitted? Mpox is not transmitted only through sexual activity. It can also spread through: Direct contact with the skin or lesions of an infected person Close contact with bodily fluids or contaminated materials Sharing clothing, towels or bedding with an infected person Prolonged close contact, including sexual contact, with an infected person The Thai Department of Disease Control emphasised that the disease is not easily spread through casual daily interactions, such as simply passing by or being near an infected person without close physical contact. Verification Process Source verification: Suchada Jiamsiri, director of the AIDS and Sexually Transmitted Infections Division at Thailand’s Department of Disease Control, said ordinary public restrooms pose a very low risk of mpox transmission and do not warrant special concern. Although the virus may be found on surfaces, contact with a contaminated surface does not necessarily result in infection. People concerned about hygiene can disinfect surfaces with alcohol and wash their hands with soap. Source verification: Thai PBS Verify interviewed Dr Anan Jongkaewwattana, director of the Innovative Animal Health and Management Research Group at the National Center for Genetic Engineering and Biotechnology. He said the risk of contracting mpox from a public toilet seat is extremely low if the skin that comes into contact with the surface has no cuts or wounds, as the virus has difficulty penetrating intact skin. However, infection remains possible if a person touches a contaminated surface and then touches their eyes, nose or mouth, where the virus can enter the body more easily through mucous membranes. Source verification: Thai PBS Verify also interviewed Emeritus Professor Amorn Leelarasamee, who said mpox can spread through contact with lesions or bodily fluids and is not limited to sexual transmission. The risk of infection increases when a person has open wounds or when the virus comes into contact with mucous membranes. When using public restrooms, people should check cleanliness, disinfect toilet seats when possible, cover any wounds, and wash their hands with soap or alcohol gel afterwards. 巴西录得2026年首例猴痘死亡病例,确诊病例突破1000例

Brazil records first mpox death of 2026, with confirmed cases surpassing 1,000
新闻简报巴西出现2026年首例猴痘死亡病例,确诊病例达到1,009例。巴西卫生部下属的菲奥克鲁斯公共卫生研究所指出,死亡事件发生在里约热内卢。9 月至 11 月期间病例有所增加,是当年剩余时间记录的病例数的两倍。当局表示,观察到的增加并不构成暴发,目前的感染数字仍远低于2022年记录的峰值。作为应对措施,巴西当局加强了疾病监测措施,并额外采购了25,000剂猴痘 疫苗。世界卫生组织(WHO)此前已宣布该疾病为2022年和2024年国际关注的突发公共卫生事件。

巴西出现 2026 年首例 猴痘 死亡病例,确诊病例突破 1000 例。9 月至 11 月期间病例有所增加,是当年剩余时间记录的病例数的两倍。

该事件发生在巴西,特别是里约热内卢市。受影响人群包括通过密切身体接触或动物与人类 猴痘 感染 猴痘 的个体。

巴西卫生部下属的菲奥克鲁斯公共卫生研究所指出,死亡事件发生在里约热内卢。

巴西猴痘确诊病例已超过1000例,并报告死亡病例。然而,当局表示,观察到的增长并不构成暴发。目前的感染数字仍远低于 2022 年记录的峰值。
应对、状态与不确定性

B routine priorityPublic-health news

Brazil records first mpox death of 2026, with confirmed cases surpassing 1,000

News Brief
Brazil has recorded its first mpox death of 2026, with confirmed cases reaching 1,009. The Fiocruz public health institute, affiliated with Brazil’s Ministry of Health, noted the death occurred in Rio de Janeiro. The increase in cases was observed between September and November, doubling the number of cases recorded during the rest of the year. Authorities have stated that the observed increase does not constitute an outbreak, and current infection figures remain well below the peak recorded in 2022. In response, Brazilian authorities have strengthened disease surveillance measures and procured an additional 25,000 doses of the mpox vaccine. The World Health Organization (WHO) previously declared the disease a public health emergency of international concern in 2022 and 2024.
Original source text (English)

World Gulf Middle East Asia Europe Africa Americas Business Qatar Business Middle East Business World Business Stock Market Energy Sports Qatar Sport Football Formula One NBA Cricket Tennis Rally Athletics Motosports Golf Horse Racing Olympics Padel Cuesports Views Editor-in-Chief Opinion Editorial Doha Today Community Campus Souq Life Style Technology Motoring Feature & more Wellness Food Travel Fashion Science & Environment Entertainment Cinema Music In Focus Qatar 2022 Doha Expo Asian Cup IAAF Doha2019 Ramadan Qatar National Day World Cup 2026 News Opinion Writers Videos Editorials Handbook World / Americas Brazil records first mpox death of 2026, with confirmed cases surpassing 1,000 Published: 15 Nov 2026 - 11:26 am | Last Updated: 15 Nov 2026 - 11:27 am Follow usnews Copy A+ A- QNA Brasilia: Brazil has recorded its first death from mpox (Monkeypox) in 2026, with the number of confirmed cases reaching 1,009. Authorities have stated that the observed increase does not constitute an outbreak. In a report, the Fiocruz public health institute-affiliated with Brazil’s Ministry of Health-noted that the death occurred in the city of Rio de Janeiro. The city has seen a rise in confirmed cases between September and November, with the total for this period doubling the number of cases recorded during the rest of the year. Brazilian authorities have strengthened disease surveillance measures and procured an additional 25,000 doses of the mpox vaccine. They noted that current infection figures remain well below the peak recorded in 2022. The mpox virus can be transmitted from animals to humans, as well as between individuals through close physical contact. It causes symptoms such as fever, muscle aches, and a skin rash, and in some cases, it can be fatal. The World Health Organization (WHO) previously declared the disease a public health emergency of international concern in both 2022 and 2024, following its rapid spread across several countries. Brazilian authorities have strengthened disease surveillance measures and procured an additional 25,000 doses of the mpox vaccine. The World Health Organization (WHO) previously declared the disease a public health emergency of international concern in 2022 and 2024.

South Sudan on alert as DRC records monkeypox cases - Eye Radio

News Brief
South Sudan has heightened its alert status in response to the mpox outbreak reported in DR Congo. Surveillance officers have been posted at the border, and the country’s health ministry has conducted training to ensure preparedness. The CDC reported over 20,000 suspected mpox cases and about 1,000 deaths in DR Congo since January 2023, with the current outbreak widespread in 25 of the country’s 26 provinces. However, few cases have been confirmed through laboratory testing. South Sudan’s health officials stated that they have not found any confirmed cases in the region, despite a suspected case in Ezra County. The surveillance team was dispatched to investigate, but no evidence of mpox was found. The ministry remains vigilant and has trained personnel to detect and respond to any potential cases.

Health South Sudan on alert as DRC records monkeypox cases Michael Daniel • June 24, 2024 • 2 min read Child manifesting symptoms of monkeypox disease|Courtesy| MoH Western Equatoria Ministry of Health and partners have deployed a surveillance team to the border with DR Congo following the reported outbreak of monkeypox disease there. Dr. Rose Obede, the state health ministry’s director general, said surveillance officers have been posted at the border in response to the mpox outbreak reported in May this year. The US Center for Disease Control (CDC) said the vast tropical country has recorded more than 20,000 suspected mpox cases and about 1,000 deaths since January 2023. CDC said the current outbreak is widespread in in 25 of the country’s 26 provinces although few have been confirmed in a laboratory testing. Meanwhile, Dr. Obede said surveillance officers recently sent to Nzara County after a child reportedly manifested symptoms related to the viral disease. The health official said the surveillance team dispatched to the area didn’t find the suspected case. “For the concerns about monkeypox in our neighboring DR Congo, we heard about it and then we have our surveillance officers. They are now notified and we have trained many more,” she said. “We have done refreshment training also last month for them to be vigilant and detect whatever comes. Up to now, we are okay and there has been nothing since we had about a (suspected case) of a small child in Ezra, but we went to see it but we didn’t get it.” According to the World Health Organization, mpox is a viral illness caused by the monkeypox virus, a species of the genus Orthopoxvirus. The Common symptoms of mpox are a skin rash or mucosal lesions which can last 2–4 weeks accompanied by fever, headache, muscle aches, back pain, low energy, and swollen lymph nodes. Mpox can be transmitted to humans through physical contact with someone infectious, with contaminated materials, or with infected animals. Anyone can get mpox and it spreads from contact with infected: persons, through touch, kissing, or sex animals, when hunting, skinning, or cooking them materials, such as contaminated sheets, clothes or needles pregnant persons, who may pass the virus on to their unborn baby. If you have mpox: Tell anyone you have been close to recently Stay at home until all scabs fall off and a new layer of skin forms Cover lesions and wear a well-fitting mask when around other people Avoid physical contact. 猴痘 错误信息研究中的 SIR 和 SEIR 模型比较 - Bioengineer.org

SIR and SEIR Models Compare in Mpox Misinformation Study - Bioengineer.org
新闻简报研究人员 Udoka Duruaku、Tawakalit Omolabake Agboola 和 Oluwaseun Ajao 进行的一项研究比较了 SIR 和 SEIR 流行病学模型,以分析社交媒体上与 2022 年 猴痘 暴发 相关的健康错误信息的传播情况。该研究使用了 2022 年 7 月 7 日至 13 日期间收集的 5,787 条推文数据集,其中大多数活动集中在 7 月 9 日。作者指出了短期数据集的局限性,并强调这些发现是方法论见解,而不是对 暴发 错误信息景观的全面分析。该研究强调了健康错误信息在数字平台上迅速传播的挑战,这可能会阻碍公共卫生沟通和应对策略。该研究强调需要更大的纵向收集的数据集,以提高未来研究中此类分析的准确性。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该研究使用 2022 年 猴痘 暴发 的数据进行,数据集包含 2022 年 7 月 7 日至 13 日期间收集的推文。文章的发布日期为 2026 年 11 月 13 日。
地点与对象
该研究重点关注全球范围内发生的2022年猴痘 暴发,但提供的证据中并未报告具体地点和人群详细信息。
事件
研究人员进行了一项概念验证研究,以评估经典流行病学模型(SIR 和 SEIR)如何捕捉社交媒体上与 猴痘 相关的健康错误信息的时间动态。该研究比较了这些模型在代表错误信息传播方面的有效性。
规模、影响与风险
该研究分析了 2022 年 猴痘 暴发 期间收集的 5,787 条推文,强调了使用短期数据集进行综合分析的挑战。该研究强调了数字平台上快速传播健康错误信息对公共卫生沟通和应对策略的风险。
应对、状态与不确定性
作者承认他们的数据集的局限性,该数据集的时间窗口很窄,大多数帖子都集中在 2022 年 7 月 9 日。他们将这项工作视为方法论见解,而不是 猴痘 错误信息的明确流行病学特征。该研究表明,未来的研究需要更大的纵向收集的数据集。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

SIR and SEIR Models Compare in Mpox Misinformation Study - Bioengineer.org

News Brief
A study conducted by researchers Udoka Duruaku, Tawakalit Omolabake Agboola, and Oluwaseun Ajao compared the SIR and SEIR epidemiological models to analyze the spread of health misinformation related to the 2022 Mpox outbreak on social media. The research utilized a dataset of 5,787 tweets collected between 7 and 13 July 2022, with most activity concentrated on 9 July. The authors noted the limitations of their short-horizon dataset and emphasized that the findings are methodological insights rather than a comprehensive analysis of the outbreak's misinformation landscape. The study highlights the challenges of health misinformation spreading rapidly on digital platforms, which can hinder public health communication and response strategies. The research underscores the need for larger, longitudinally collected datasets to improve the accuracy of such analyses in future studies.
Original source text (English)
HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER Monday, November 16, 2026 No Result View All Result Login HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER No Result View All Result No Result View All Result Home NEWS Science News Health SIR and SEIR Models Compare in Mpox Misinformation Study by November 13, 2026 in Health Reading Time: 2 mins read 0 SIR and SEIR Models Compare in Mpox Misinformation Study Share on FacebookShare on TwitterShare on LinkedinShare on RedditShare on Telegram Researchers Udoka Duruaku, Tawakalit Omolabake Agboola, and Oluwaseun Ajao conducted a proof-of-concept study to evaluate how classical epidemiological models capture the temporal dynamics of health misinformation on social media. Using the 2022 Mpox outbreak as a case study, the team compared the Susceptible-Infectious-Recovered (SIR) and adapted Susceptible-Exposed-Infectious-Recovered (SEIR) models. The study aimed to determine which compartmental framework better represents the spread of misinformation during a disease outbreak, treating the dissemination of false health information analogously to the spread of infectious diseases within communities. The analysis utilized a publicly available dataset comprising 5,787 tweets collected between 7 and 13 July 2022. A significant limitation of this dataset is its narrow temporal window, with the vast majority of posts concentrated on a single day, 9 July 2022. Due to this concentrated activity and short observation period, the authors framed the work as an illustration of how compartmental models can be adapted to short-horizon social media bursts, rather than a definitive epidemiological characterisation of Mpox misinformation. This scope acknowledges that the findings are methodological insights rather than a comprehensive longitudinal analysis of the outbreak’s misinformation landscape. The comparative analysis revealed distinct strengths for each model in capturing different phases of misinformation spread. The SIR model effectively captured the rapid initial dissemination patterns characteristic of viral misinformation spread. In contrast, the SEIR model more accurately represented the latency period between exposure and active sharing behaviour observed in social media contexts. Discover more Check Symptoms Read Med Journals Track Outbreaks Learn Math The authors discussed the sensitivity and identifiability limits of these parameters under the short observation window. These findings contribute methodological insights for compartmental model selection in misinformation research. The authors motivate future work with larger, longitudinally collected datasets to overcome the limitations of the short-horizon data used in this proof-of-concept study. The research underscores the challenges posed by the rapid dissemination of health misinformation through digital platforms to public health communication and response strategies. 可能的 猴痘 曝光关闭了日本主要海军基地的保龄球中心 - 星条旗

Possible mpox exposure closes bowling center on major Navy base in Japan - Stars and Stripes
新闻简报日本横须贺海军基地的保龄球中心于 2026 年 11 月 5 日关闭,原因是一名来访的水手可能感染了 猴痘。该基地的士气、福利和娱乐计划于 2026 年 11 月 12 日宣布关闭,作为一项预防措施,而不是由于已确认的传播。该水手于下午 6 点左右访问了该中心。11 月 4 日,第二天报告症状。该基地表示,猴痘 主要通过密切的皮肤接触传播,在保龄球等公共场所的短暂接触被认为风险较低。来访的船上报告了两例相关病例,但横须贺海军基地社区中尚未发现确诊病例。通过接触者追踪确定密切接触者,提供疫苗接种,并进行日常医学监测。一旦消毒完成并经预防医学人员清理后,该中心将重新开放,但没有具体说明重新开放的日期。基地发言人没有回应置评请求。

事件

B routine priorityPublic-health news

Possible mpox exposure closes bowling center on major Navy base in Japan - Stars and Stripes

News Brief
The Bowling Center at Yokosuka Naval Base in Japan was closed on November 5, 2026, following a possible mpox exposure by a visiting sailor. The closure was announced on November 12, 2026, by the base’s Morale, Welfare and Recreation program as a precautionary measure, not due to confirmed spread. The sailor visited the center between approximately 6 p.m. on November 4 and reported symptoms the next day. The base stated that mpox spreads mainly through close, skin-to-skin contact and that brief contact in a public setting like bowling is considered low risk. Two related cases were reported aboard the visiting ship, but no confirmed cases have been identified among the Yokosuka Naval Base community. Close contacts were identified through contact tracing, offered vaccinations, and placed under daily medical monitoring. The center will reopen once disinfection is complete and cleared by preventive medicine personnel, though a reopening date was not specified. Base spokespeople did not respond to requests for comment.

Events Follow Stars and Stripes Pacific Facebook X Instagram Visit Stripes Printshop Asia-Pacific Possible mpox exposure closes bowling center on major Navy base in Japan The Bowling Center at Yokosuka Naval Base, Japan, was shuttered Nov. 5, 2026, following a visiting sailor's potential mpox exposure. (Alex Wilson/Stars and Stripes) By ALEX WILSON STARS AND STRIPES Published November 12, 2026 · Updated November 12, 2026 YOKOSUKA NAVAL BASE, Japan — The bowling alley at the home of the U.S. 7th Fleet is closed until further notice for deep cleaning and sanitizing following a possible mpox exposure, according to the base’s Morale, Welfare and Recreation program. A service member from a visiting ship visited the Bowling Center between approximately 6 p.m. and 8 p.m. on Nov. 4 and reported symptoms the following day, MWR wrote Thursday on its Facebook page. The center was “closed immediately, out of an abundance of caution, not because additional spread has been detected,” according to the post. Mpox, formerly known as monkey pox, is a viral illness that can cause a painful rash, enlarged lymph nodes, fever, headache, pain and low energy, according to the World Health Organization website. Most people fully recover, but some get very sick, due to infection and other complications. “Mpox does not spread easily,” the command said. “It spreads mainly through close, personal, often skin-to-skin contact, and less commonly through shared objects or fabrics.” Brief contact in a public setting, such as bowling on a nearby lane, is considered a low health risk. MWR announced the bowling alley’s closure on Nov. 5 via a separate Facebook post, but a reason was not initially provided. Two related cases have been reported aboard the ship over the past two weeks, but no confirmed cases have appeared among the Yokosuka Naval Base community, according to the post. The Bowling Center at Yokosuka Naval Base, Japan, was shuttered Nov. 5, 2026, following a visiting sailor's potential mpox exposure. (Alex Wilson/Stars and Stripes) Thursday’s announcement did not identify the visiting ship or specify whether the service member who visited the bowling center was one of the two confirmed cases. Both people with confirmed infections were isolated immediately upon showing symptoms, according to the command. Close contacts were identified through contract tracing, offered vaccinations and placed under daily medical monitoring, it stated. The bowling center will reopen once disinfection is complete and preventive medicine personnel clear it to resume operations. The announcement did not provide a reopening date. Base spokespeople did not immediately respond to email and phone requests for comment Thursday morning. Anyone who visited the bowling center on the evening of Nov. 4 should monitor for symptoms through Nov. 25, MWR said. Anyone who develops symptoms should call the U.S. Naval Hospital Yokosuka advice line before seeking in-person care and mention the possible exposure, MWR said in its post. The number is 070-7601-5392 from a Japanese mobile phone or +81-70-7601-5392 when calling internationally. 猴痘解读

Monkeypox explainer
新闻简报猴痘是由猴痘病毒引起的疾病,该病毒是引起天花的天花病毒的近缘病原体。猴痘与天花感染可引发相似症状,但猴痘的临床表现通常较轻。
查看新闻五要素
时间

Monkeypox explainer

News Brief
Monkeypox is a disease caused by the monkeypox virus, a pathogen closely related to variola virus, which causes smallpox. Monkeypox and smallpox infections trigger similar symptoms, but monkeypox.

【文献阅读】Nat. Microbiol.|人皮肤类器官模拟猴痘病毒感染并用于抗病毒药物评价
News Brief
The supplied evidence does not clearly report the event time in verified English. Mpox virus infection and drug treatment modelled in human skin organoids. The supplied evidence does not clearly report the central event in verified English. The supplied evidence does not clearly report the scale, impact, or risk in verified English. The supplied source does not clearly report the response status or unresolved information.
Original source text (Chinese)
【文献阅读】Nat. Microbiol.|人皮肤类器官模拟猴痘病毒感染并用于抗病毒药物评价 病毒学界 2026-11-14 12:31发布于北京 问AI · 特考韦瑞早期有效延迟欠佳对临床治疗窗有何暗示? 2022 年,猴痘(mpox)疫情首次在非洲以外的 100 多个国家/地区大规模暴发,被世界卫生组织宣布为"国际关注的突发公共卫生事件"。与天花病毒同属正痘病毒属的猴痘病毒(MPXV)最典型的临床表现恰恰是皮肤病变——生殖器肛周部位皮损在 2022 年疫情中尤为常见,部分患者还出现剧烈疼痛、全身播散与继发细菌感染。然而,MPXV 研究领域长期缺乏能真实重现人皮肤感染的实验模型:现有体外模型全部基于永生化细胞系,无法概括人皮肤的多层结构与附属器;多种动物模型虽支持病毒复制,却通常不出现人类患者所见的皮肤表现。模型缺位直接制约了病毒—宿主互作研究与药物评价。 2023 年 10 月 12 日,荷兰伊拉斯姆斯大学医学中心 Qiuwei Pan 团队联合莱顿大学医学中心 Karine Raymond 团队在 Nature Microbiology 发表研究论文(Nat. Microbiol. 2023;8:2067–2079),系统评估了人诱导多能干细胞(hiPS)来源皮肤类器官对 MPXV 的支持能力。该团队以 2022 年暴发疫情的皮疹来源临床分离株(European Virus Archive,010V-04721)攻击三个分化阶段(第 55 天毛芽期、第 90 天毛钉期、第 130 天含毛囊终末期)的皮肤类器官及气—液界面(ALI)培养体系,从病毒复制动力学、超微结构、转录组与药物干预四个层面建立了迄今最接近人皮肤生理的 MPXV 感染与药物评价模型。 图1. 早期(第 55 天)皮肤类器官支持 MPXV 感染(原文 Fig.1)。a:类器官明场图像;b–d:细胞内与培养上清中病毒基因组拷贝数 7 天内升高约 3 个数量级,上清感染性滴度由约 1.5–2 log10 升至 3.7 log10 PFU/mL;e:dsRNA 与 KRT5/KRT15 共定位证实角化上皮细胞受染;f–h:透射电镜(TEM)显示以未成熟与成熟病毒体为主的胞内病毒颗粒及中间装配形态。 发现一:从毛芽到含毛囊终末期,各阶段皮肤类器官均支持 MPXV 生产性感染 hiPS 皮肤类器官的一个显著特点是"倒置"构型——真皮层朝外接触培养环境、表皮层朝内,并带有毛囊、皮脂腺等附属结构。研究以约 105 PFU 的病毒攻击后,三个独立 hiPS 细胞系的感染动力学完全一致,后续结果合并分析。第 55 天早期类器官中,细胞内病毒基因组 7 天升高 3 个 log10,上清病毒 DNA 持续攀升并于 7 天达约 9 log10 拷贝/mL,感染性滴度同步升高;免疫染色显示病毒复制中间体双链 RNA(dsRNA)与基底/中间层角蛋白 KRT5、KRT15 共定位于同一细胞,直接锁定角化上皮细胞为支持感染的主要细胞类型。第 90 天晚期类器官复现了相似结果:分泌型病毒滴度从 1 小时的 2.4 log10 升至 7 天的 5 log10 PFU/mL;全器官包埋免疫染色在 KRT5 阳性区域广泛检出病毒 dsRNA。第 130 天终末期类器官已形成完整毛囊结构,接种后同样呈现约 3 个 log10 的复制增长与约 2 个 log10 的分泌与滴度增长,表皮面 MPXV 与基底膜标志物整合素 β4 共染清晰定位感染灶。 图2. 晚期(第 90 天)皮肤类器官中的 MPXV 感染与病毒颗粒(原文 Fig.2)。b–d:细胞内与上清病毒载量及感染性滴度动态;e–h:全器官包埋与冷冻切片免疫染色显示 KRT5 阳性细胞中的病毒 dsRNA;i:TEM 显示基底层角化细胞内大量病毒体(可见基底膜、半桥粒等特征结构)。 发现二:透射电镜完整捕捉痘病毒胞内装配的四个阶段 参照痘苗病毒的经典认识,正痘病毒胞内装配依次经历月牙形前体(crescent)、未成熟病毒体(IV)、成熟病毒体(MV)与经高尔基体/内体包裹的包裹型病毒体(WV)四个阶段,但 MPXV 的胞内装配此前缺乏直观的超微结构证据。本研究在终末期类器官的 TEM 分析中完整捕获了这四个相继阶段,并观察到病毒工厂的纤维状形态、病毒浆进入未成熟病毒体的动态过程,以及感染晚期出现的细胞降解;在 ALI 培养体系中同样捕获了未成熟病毒体正在形成的瞬间。值得一提的是,近期对痘苗病毒的研究在经典两型(未成熟/成熟)之外还描述了多种中间形态,本研究在皮肤类器官中观察到的装配中间步骤为痘病毒形态发生研究提供了新的组织学背景。 图3. 终末期(第 130 天,含毛囊)皮肤类器官中的 MPXV 感染(原文 Fig.3)。b–d:病毒载量与感染性滴度增长;e–f:全器官包埋与单层光切显示 MPXV(绿)与整合素 β4(红)共定位;g:TEM 完整呈现月牙体、未成熟病毒体(IV)、成熟病毒体(MV)与包裹型病毒体(WV)四个装配阶段;h:角化细胞内的病毒颗粒。 发现三:转录组层面——病毒基因大量表达与宿主皮肤稳态失衡 作为大型双链 DNA 病毒,MPXV 可转录数百个基因。对第 90 天类器官的转录组分析显示,接种后 1 小时病毒基因几乎不表达,至第 7 天大量病毒转录本密集映射于参考基因组各位置。宿主方面,主成分分析清晰呈现感染后转录组的整体重塑;基因本体分析显示免疫应答、自噬与抗病毒通路显著上调,而皮肤发育与角质化通路显著下调——提示感染扰乱了类器官的皮肤稳态。基因集富集分析进一步指向自噬、凋亡、坏死性凋亡与铁死亡。在单基因层面,与皮肤病理相关的上皮源细胞因子 TSLP 与 IL-36γ 于感染第 7 天显著上调;更值得注意的是,棘层标志物 KRT1/KRT10 及皮肤屏障相关基因(FLG、FLG2、DSG1、DSC1)大幅下降,而基底细胞标志物 ITGA6、ITGB4、KRT14 反而上调,呈现"基底层化"的去分化特征,提示感染可能损害类器官的皮肤屏障功能。 图4. MPXV 感染类器官的转录组分析(原文 Fig.4)。a–b:病毒转录本定位与相对丰度热图(7 天时大量表达,特考韦瑞治疗组显著回落);c:PCA 显示感染重塑宿主转录组、治疗组与未感染对照聚在一起;d:基因本体富集;e:TSLP/IL-36γ 上调、棘层与屏障基因下调、基底标志物上调,特考韦瑞处理使上述变化回到基线。 发现四:特考韦瑞早期给药有效,延迟给药效力大打折扣 特考韦瑞(tecovirimat)是 FDA 批准治疗天花的抗病毒药物,通过抑制病毒包膜蛋白 VP37 阻断细胞外病毒形成,已被探索用于 MPXV 治疗但临床疗效尚无定论。研究首先模拟早期给药场景(接种后 1 小时),设置了 0.1 μM 至临床可达血药浓度(1、5 μM)的梯度:病毒 DNA 分泌在 96 小时即被显著抑制,7 天时抑制作用进一步增强;1 或 5 μM 处理 7 天后空斑实验检测不到感染性病毒,细胞内病毒 DNA 下降约 2 个 log10;病毒转录本占比由未治疗的 40% 骤降至 0.85%;宿主转录组重塑被完全阻止——治疗样本在 PCA 上与未感染对照聚为一类,TSLP/IL-36G 回落至基线,棘层与屏障基因所受抑制被解除。延迟给药场景则结果迥异:第 4 天才开始以 1 μM 给药,第 6–10 天(对应给药后 2–6 天)上清病毒 DNA 未见抑制,第 12、14 天仅轻度下降;感染性滴度在给药后 6 天才出现约 2 个 log10 的下降。作者援引一项纳入 49 例美国患者的观察——症状出现至首次给予特考韦瑞的中位时间为 7 天——指出早期启动治疗在临床上具有现实意义,这一延迟给药模型为临床治疗窗的界定提供了直接的实验参照。 图5. 特考韦瑞早期与延迟治疗的效果(原文 Fig.5)。a–f:早期给药(1 hpi)显著抑制病毒 DNA 分泌与感染性病毒产生,1/5 μM 组 7 天空斑检测阴性,病毒转录本占比 40%→0.85%;g:治疗阻止宿主转录组重塑;h–j:延迟给药(4 dpi)仅呈轻度且迟发的抑制作用。 发现五:ALI 培养体系进一步贴近人皮肤生理,"从上皮面接种、经真皮面给药" 气—液界面培养是皮肤类器官技术的最新进展:类器官被解剖后种植于胶原包被的 transwell 上,真皮面接触培养基、表皮面暴露于空气,成熟后形成带有毛发毛囊、更接近人皮肤生理的结构。本研究将 MPXV 接种于表皮面,模拟病毒经皮肤上皮入侵的自然途径,真皮侧培养基中病毒分泌持续增加(7 天逾 2 个 log10),感染性滴度升至 3 log10 PFU/mL。特考韦瑞经培养基给药以模拟体内药物吸收:7 天治疗后分泌病毒基因组下降约 2 个 log10,未治疗组 3 log10 PFU/mL 的感染性病毒在治疗组中完全检测不到。H&E 染色与免疫染色证实 ALI 类器官的分层结构(基底 KRT5、棘层 KRT10、颗粒层 LOR)与人皮肤高度相似,TEM 则观察到角蛋白束、桥粒、胶原纤维等典型皮肤结构及四个装配阶段的病毒体。至此,从早期到终末期、从游离到 ALI,模型体系完整覆盖了 MPXV—皮肤互作研究的主要需求。 图6. ALI 培养皮肤类器官中的 MPXV 感染与抗病毒治疗(原文 Fig.6)。b–e:表皮面接种后病毒自真皮面持续分泌,特考韦瑞经培养基给药使感染性病毒完全检测不出;f–g:H&E 与免疫染色显示类器官分层结构与人皮肤相似、病毒颗粒分布于表皮与真皮层;h–i:TEM 显示未成熟病毒体形成过程及四个装配阶段。 局限与展望 作者对模型边界作了明确说明:皮肤类器官虽重现了表皮分层与附属器结构,但缺失免疫细胞组分,无法反映感染局部的免疫应答;类器官的"倒置"构型与人皮肤的生理朝向仍有差异,ALI 体系对此有所改进但尚未完全解决。此外,本研究使用单一临床分离株,不同分支毒株的复制与致病差异有待扩展;特考韦瑞的作用机制(阻断细胞外病毒)与观察到的"延迟给药效力下降"之间的因果关系,亦需在模型中进一步解析。作者展望,未来可引入相关免疫细胞群体、利用患者来源的 hiPS 细胞构建个体化模型,并将该体系用于 MPXV 致病机制(如皮肤炎症与免疫病理)与新药、联用方案(如特考韦瑞与布林西多福韦)的系统性评价。 综之,这项研究将 hiPS 皮肤类器官确立为 MPXV 研究的稳健模型:三个分化阶段与 ALI 体系均支持病毒生产性感染,TEM 完整捕获四阶段装配,转录组揭示病毒基因大量表达与皮肤屏障相关程序的下调,特考韦瑞的早期/延迟给药差异与 ALI 给药模拟为临床治疗窗提供了实验参照。对于以皮肤病变为核心表现的 mpox 而言,这一"能够感染、能够成像、能够给药"的人源模型,恰补上了病毒—宿主互作研究与抗病毒评价的关键一环。 论文信息:Li P, Pachis ST, Xu G, et al. Mpox virus infection and drug treatment modelled in human skin organoids. Nat Microbiol 2023;8(11):2067–2079

Original title (Chinese)
泰国芭提雅夜场启动猴痘防疫宣传,回应员工与游客感染担忧
News Brief
The supplied evidence does not clearly report the event time in verified English. The supplied source does not clearly identify the location or affected population. The supplied evidence does not clearly report the central event in verified English. The supplied source does not clearly report the scale, impact, or risk. The supplied source does not clearly report the response status or unresolved information.
Original source text (Chinese)
泰国芭提雅夜场启动猴痘防疫宣传,回应员工与游客感染担忧 泰国中文社 2026-11-10 11:04发布于广东国际领域创作者 问AI · 杯具染痘引不安,夜场如何破局稳口碑? 芭提雅公共卫生人员与私营机构合作,面向娱乐场所经营者和员工启动猴痘(mpox)防疫宣传项目。此前有关感染的担忧在当地商界引发不安,外界担心可能影响芭提雅的夜场生意。 这一项目是在一名泰国男子于Facebook发布自己感染猴痘的视频后推出的。他称自己并非经性行为感染,怀疑是在派对上与陌生人共用杯子时接触病毒。该帖引发社交媒体用户担忧,经常使用公共交通和公厕的人以及娱乐场所员工也在其中。 芭提雅面向外国游客提供服务的经营者同样关注疫情传播。部分业者担心,把感染与共用杯子联系起来的报道可能损害场所声誉,令顾客不愿参加派对。 芭提雅夜生活商业协会主席Lamphueng Hamilton表示,一些经营者担心病毒可能在员工和顾客之间传播。她称,芭提雅市和邦拉蒙县的公共卫生人员已进入娱乐场所,讲解猴痘的传播方式、向员工提供防护建议,并持续开展监测。 Lamphueng Hamilton补充说,政府机构、私营企业以及SWING基金会和Sisters基金会两家非营利组织共同参与了该项目。这些团体重点支持需要与大量顾客近距离接触的员工,并为怀疑自己感染的人提供咨询,内容包括治疗、隔离以及同事应如何对待可能感染的员工。 在芭提雅工作的50岁按摩从业者Thanam Khamphanut表示,由于工作需要与顾客近距离接触,她对感染感到担忧。但她相信,通过适当防护可以降低风险,因此她勤洗手、戴口罩,并小心避免密切接触。 泰国医学厅指出,猴痘可通过与患者皮疹、水疱、伤口或黏膜的密切接触传播,性行为、接吻和直接皮肤接触均可能造成传播。病毒还可经由被污染的毛巾、衣物和床品,以及密切接触时的呼吸道分泌物传播;在没有这类接触的情况下同处一个空间,不视为高风险传播途径。 疾病控制厅厅长Montien Kanasawat表示,普通验血无法确认猴痘感染,诊断须从皮肤病灶采样进行PCR检测,因为猴痘病灶可能与疱疹或水痘病灶相似。首次检测结果为阴性但仍持续有症状的人,应返回就医作进一步评估。 Montien Kanasawat还呼吁公众不要污名化猴痘患者。担心自己接触过病毒或出现症状的人,可拨打疾病控制厅热线1422寻求建议。 疾病控制厅记录显示,截至2026年10月31日,泰国累计报告猴痘病例470例、死亡3例。世界卫生组织9月14日发布的报告则显示,2025年1月至2026年8月底,107个国家累计确诊68,096例、死亡272例。 泰国中文社11月10日推送
